- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Case reports in hematology
- 作者: Elhasan HEA, Merghani T, Ahmed F, Hamid SM, Alawad A
- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.1155/crh/4650998
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43005851/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-11

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Immune thrombotic thrombocytopenic purpura (iTTP) is a rare but fatal disorder characterized by microangiopathic hemolytic anemia, severe thrombocytopenia, and ischemic damage of organs due to widespread platelet-rich thrombi in small blood vessels. Early detection and prompt treatment are crucial to reducing mortality.
CASE PRESENTATION: We report a case of a female, 66-year-old, who presented with transient episode of left-sided weakness and aphasia that resolved within 30 min. Initial neuroimaging showed no evidence of ischemia. Laboratory evaluation showed severe thrombocytopenia, severe anemia, high lactate dehydrogenase (LDH), reticulocytosis, and the presence of schistocytes in peripheral blood. The calculated PLASMIC score was 6, and ADAMTS13 activity showed severe deficiency, supporting the diagnosis of iTTP. The patient responded well to corticosteroids and therapeutic plasma exchange, with significant clinical and hematological improvement following the treatment. She was discharged after 2 weeks in good condition with a plan for regular follow-up.
CONCLUSION: This case emphasizes the significance of including iTTP in the differential diagnosis of transient neurological attacks in patients with hematological abnormalities. Early initiation of plasma exchange and immunosuppressive therapy are essential to prevent organ damage and further complications.

### [A] Heavy Metal Toxicity Following Metal-on-Metal Knee Arthroplasty Revision Surgery: A Case Report.

- PMID: 43005844
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence

- DOI: 10.1155/cro/3777088
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43005844/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-11

#### 摘要

Metallosis is a rare complication; hence, previous investigations regarding this topic are restricted to case reports. Little has been published on metallosis following total knee arthroplasty (TKA). We report the case of a patient who experienced cobalt and chromium metallosis 14 years following TKA-revision surgery. She developed a variety of symptoms that could indicate systemic clinical toxicity, including skin redness, headaches, irritability, cognitive decline, and sleep disturbances. Replacing the prosthesis, utilizing chelation therapy, and trials of double-filtration plasmapheresis reduced the serum level of heavy metals upon regular laboratory follow-ups, leading to a temporary relief of symptoms. The presence of confounding factors and other concomitant conditions makes the distinction between systemic metal exposure and suspected systemic clinical toxicity very challenging.

### [A] Progressive Ataxia and Cognitive Impairment Associated With SEZ6L2: Serum Neurofilament Light Chain as a Biomarker of Disease Activity.

- PMID: 43006825
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Brain and behavior
- 作者: Fazio C, Regalbuto S, Arceri S, Comolli D, Calculli A, Trobbiani G, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov
- DOI: 10.1002/brb3.71900
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43006825/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-11

#### 摘要

INTRODUCTION: Autoimmune encephalitis (AE) associated with SEZ6L2 antibodies is a rare neurological disorder characterized by subacute onset of cerebellar ataxia, cognitive dysfunction, and extrapyramidal features. Few cases have been reported, and data on long-term course and treatment response remain limited.
CASE PRESENTATION: A 64-year-old woman developed progressive cerebellar ataxia, cognitive decline, and psychiatric symptoms starting insidiously after SARS-CoV-2 pneumonia in September 2022. Magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) and cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) analysis almost 1 year after onset were unremarkable. Due to progressive symptoms, she was admitted to our department in December 2024. Examination showed severe truncal and gait ataxia, memory impairment, and prominent frontal-executive dysfunction. Brain MRI demonstrated bilateral hippocampal T2 hyperintensity with severe medial temporal lobe and cerebellar atrophy. Neuronal antibody testing with a tissue-based assay (TBA) optimized for surface antigens showed strong neuropilar hippocampal and cerebellar staining in serum and CSF. A specific live cell-based assay (CBA) confirmed that the reactivity could be attributed to SEZ6L2 antibodies. Plasmapheresis and high-dose corticosteroids resulted in stabilization of gait impairment and mild cognitive improvement. Longitudinal plasma neurofilament light chain (NFL) monitoring showed initially high levels (85.12 pg/mL), followed by a drastic reduction some months after immunotherapy.
CONCLUSIONS: AE can mimic neurodegenerative processes, and SEZ6L2 antibody testing should be considered in patients with progressive ataxia and limbic involvement, even when no additional clues point towards an autoimmune etiology. Notably, the condition manifested a partial response to immunotherapy, supported by NFL reduction. This suggests that NFL might represent a useful biomarker to monitor disease activity in these conditions.

### [A] Neonatal bilirubin neurotoxicity: An emergency response framework from referral to follow-up.

- PMID: 43009375
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Seminars in perinatology
- 作者: Gutmann D, Parsons A, Sankar MN
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 10
- DOI: 10.1016/j.semperi.2026.152328
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43009375/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-12

#### 摘要

A neonate with a total serum bilirubin at or approaching the exchange transfusion threshold represents a life-threatening neurologic emergency that demands a coordinated, time-critical response. The 2022 American Academy of Pediatrics clinical practice guideline revision formally defines an escalation-of-care threshold at 2 mg/dL below the exchange transfusion threshold to trigger prompt, aggressive therapeutic interventions. However, the vast majority of extreme hyperbilirubinemia cases undergo triage in low-resource or community settings, such as outpatient pediatric clinics, general emergency departments, or level I/II nurseries. These settings often lack the specialized capabilities required to perform an emergency exchange transfusion. Consequently, clinical outcomes and long-term neurodevelopmental prognoses depend on rapid recognition of transfer criteria, immediate initiation of temporizing management at the referring site, and stabilization during transport. This review has been developed at an academic level IV neonatal intensive care unit (NICU) and translates AAP guidelines into an actionable, role-specific protocol for referring clinicians, transport teams, receiving NICU staff, and institutional quality bodies. Emphasis is placed on immediate treatment at first clinical contact, specifically continuous intensive phototherapy and intravenous hydration within minutes rather than delaying until arrival at a higher-level center, alongside parallel exchange transfusion preparation, standardized neurologic assessment, proactive family counseling, and institutional follow-up.

### [A] Challenges and limitations of mobilization and stem cell collection for gene therapy of sickle cell disease.

#### 摘要

Sickle cell disease (SCD) is a hemolytic anemia that afflicts millions of people worldwide and continues to carry high morbidity and reduced life expectancy. Allogeneic hematopoietic stem cell transplantation was the only standard-of-care option for cure before the US Food and Drug Administration's approval of 2 cellular drugs. Transfusion medicine plays a pivotal role in supporting patients through mobilization and apheresis collection of peripheral blood stem cells and through transplant using standard or exchange transfusion strategies. Despite these advances, obtaining sufficient cells to generate a cellular gene therapy product and lack of standardized protocols that describe the optimal preparative regimen, mobilization, and collection of stem cells present a significant barrier to success. Our working group, convened as part of the National Heart, Lung, and Blood Institute-funded Cure Sickle Cell Initiative, sought to identify gaps in our understanding of these processes to improve advanced cell therapies for SCD.

### [A] Anti-glomerular basement membrane disease-treatment standard.

- PMID: 40973182
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Nephrology, dialysis, transplantation : official publication of the European Dialysis and Transplant Association - European Renal Association
- 作者: McAdoo SP, Pusey CD
- 发表日期: 2025 Dec 23
- DOI: 10.1093/ndt/gfaf190
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/40973182/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-12

#### 摘要

We review the current standards of treatment and discuss novel developments in the pathophysiology, diagnosis, outcome prediction and management of anti-glomerular basement membrane (anti-GBM) disease. Anti-GBM disease is a unique form of small vessel vasculitis affecting the glomerular and pulmonary capillaries. It is caused by autoantibodies directed against the α3 chain of type IV collagen, leading to rapidly progressive glomerulonephritis with pulmonary haemorrhage in ∼50% of cases. Diagnosis relies on clinical features, kidney biopsy showing linear IgG deposition along the GBM, and/or detection of circulating anti-GBM antibodies. Historically, untreated disease was rapidly fatal, but the introduction of plasma exchange combined with cyclophosphamide and glucocorticoids has significantly improved outcomes, particularly in patients who are not dialysis-dependent at presentation. Dialysis-dependent patients have a lower likelihood of renal recovery, and treatment decisions must consider biopsy findings, clinical severity and potential contraindications to standard immunosuppression. Unlike anti-neutrophil cytoplasm antibody (ANCA)-associated vasculitis, relapses are rare in classic anti-GBM disease, and long-term maintenance immunosuppression is not routinely required. However, 'double positive' patients (anti-GBM and ANCA) have a higher relapse risk and require maintenance immunosuppressive treatment. Atypical anti-GBM presentations, including seronegative cases, are now better recognized but their optimal management remains unclear. Future research should define the use of oral versus intravenous cyclophosphamide in anti-GBM disease, clarify the role of rituximab and determine the place of emerging therapies such as imlifidase. Advances in risk stratification and ongoing trials are expected to inform treatment individualization and to improve treatment approaches for this aggressive autoimmune disease.

### [A] Intravenous immunoglobulin and exchange transfusion in severe iso-immune hemolytic hyperbilirubinemia: A retrospective cohort study.

- PMID: 43024821
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory

- 作者: Fahmy M, Aljohani A, Al Faleh A, Alhazmi F, Almohammadi H, Khoyate A, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 13
- DOI: 10.1177/19345798261506556
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43024821/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-14

#### 摘要

ObjectivesTo assess Intravenous Immunoglobulin (IVIG) efficacy in preventing exchange transfusion (ET) in neonates with iso-immune hemolytic disease of the newborn (HDN).Study DesignA retrospective data review of 22 688 neonates admitted to the NICU of Madinah Maternity and Children's Hospital, KSA from January 2015 to January 2025 was done. Out of them, 173 neonates ≥35 weeks with HDN at risk for ET were identified based on American Academy of Pediatrics (AAP) 2022 thresholds (74 received IVIG and 99 did not). Infants were managed according to 2004 AAP guidelines. We assessed the following outcomes: rate of ET, occurrence of rebound hyperbilirubinemia, phototherapy (PT) duration, length of hospital stay, and the need for packed red blood cells (PRBC) transfusion. The association of IVIG with outcomes was adjusted to quintiles of propensity score (PS) controlling for the likelihood of receiving IVIG.ResultsIVIG recipients had a greater hemolytic burden (initial hemoglobin: 14.19 vs. 16.06 g/dL, p < 0.001; reticulocyte count: 11.31% vs. 8.75%, p = 0.002; positive direct antiglobulin test: 94.6% vs. 80.8%, p = 0.008). Unadjusted analyses showed that IVIG recipients, compared to the non-IVIG group, had higher ET rates (20.27% vs. 5.05%; OR 4.78 [1.65-13.84], p = 0.002), longer PT duration, extended hospital stays, and more PRBC transfusions. However, after propensity score adjustment, no significant differences were observed for ET and PRBC transfusions. Consistent differences were retained for PT and hospital stay durations.ConclusionIVIG as an adjunct to intensive PT was not associated with a statistically significant reduction in ET rates in infants with HDN.

### [A] A case report of neonatal Rh hemolytic disease caused by anti-c and anti-E antibodies.

- PMID: 43024282
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Medicine
- 作者: Li X, Han Q, Zhao H, Li K, Ma Y
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 13
- DOI: 10.1097/MD.0000000000051436
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43024282/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-14

#### 摘要

RATIONALE: This article reports a case of neonatal Rhesus (Rh) hemolytic disease presenting with diffuse generalized petechiae as the initial symptom. Laboratory investigations ultimately confirmed that the mother, who had a history of prior blood transfusions and pregnancies, had developed anti-c and anti-E antibodies, leading to severe hemolytic manifestations in the neonate at an early stage. The initial clinical presentation of hemolysis in this case is relatively uncommon, and since the infant and mother shared the same ABO blood type, hemolytic disease of the newborn caused by rare Rh antibodies arising from maternal pregnancy or transfusion history is often overlooked in clinical practice. Therefore, this case is worthy of reporting to alert frontline clinicians.
PATIENT CONCERNS: Early in the course, the infant presented with diffuse generalized petechiae, generalized jaundice, and thrombocytopenia.
DIAGNOSES: A series of serological tests were performed, including direct antiglobulin test, antibody screening, elution test, and maternal/infant antibody titer determinations, ultimately leading to a diagnosis of hemolytic disease of the newborn mediated by both anti-c and anti-E antibodies.
INTERVENTIONS: During treatment, because the family refused exchange transfusion, the infant received only intensive phototherapy, intravenous immunoglobulin, albumin infusions, and multiple blood transfusions.
OUTCOMES: Owing to timely diagnosis and active treatment, the infant had a favorable prognosis.
LESSONS: This report aims to emphasize that in neonates presenting with early hyperbilirubinemia and a maternal history of blood transfusion, the possibility of rare Rh antibodies should be considered. Furthermore, it supports the implementation of routine nonspecific antibody screening for pregnant women at high risk.

### [A] Case Report: Fulminant sintilimab-associated myocarditis complicated by complete atrioventricular block and suspected myositis-myasthenia gravis overlap syndrome.

- PMID: 43021877
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Frontiers in cardiovascular medicine
- 作者: Cheng P, Zhao X, Jin M, Lai Q, Liu J
- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.3389/fcvm.2026.1968961
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43021877/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-14

#### 摘要

Immune checkpoint inhibitor (ICI)-associated myocarditis can cause life-threatening conduction disturbances and may coexist with neuromuscular immune-related adverse events. We report a 67-year-old man with stage IIIA lung squamous cell carcinoma who developed fatigue, bilateral ptosis, myalgia, weakness, and palpitations 13 days after his first dose of sintilimab following concurrent chemoradiotherapy. On admission, cardiac troponin T (cTnT) was 874.2 ng/L and creatine kinase-MB exceeded 300 μg/L. Serial electrocardiograms showed progression from bundle branch and fascicular block to complete atrioventricular block, profound bradycardia, and shock, despite a left ventricular ejection fraction of 54%. Coronary angiography revealed no obstructive coronary disease. The findings supported a clinical diagnosis of sintilimab-associated myocarditis with clinically suspected myositis-myasthenia gravis overlap syndrome; cardiac magnetic resonance imaging and confirmatory neuromuscular investigations were not performed. Treatment included intravenous methylprednisolone, immunoglobulin, mycophenolate mofetil, temporary cardiac pacing, and two limited-volume plasma exchange sessions (600 mL each). Infliximab (300 mg per infusion) was subsequently administered empirically for persistent myocardial biomarker elevation. Sinus rhythm returned, and cardiopulmonary and neuromuscular symptoms improved. Following withdrawal of immunosuppression, chest discomfort recurred alongside a cTnT rise to 299.4 ng/L, prompting resumption of corticosteroid-based therapy. At the latest follow-up on April 30, 2026, the patient remained asymptomatic with stable cardiac function, although cTnT remained mildly elevated. In ICI-associated myocarditis, a preserved ejection fraction does not exclude life-threatening conduction disease, and myocardial injury may recur after immunosuppression is withdrawn; continued ECG and troponin surveillance is therefore warranted.

### [A] Fatal Hepatitis B Virus Reactivation Following Immunosuppressive Therapy in a Patient with an Atypical Hepatitis B Virus Serological Profile: A Case Report.

- PMID: 43022442
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory

- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Journal of clinical and translational hepatology
- 作者: Liu F, Fu X, Yu H, Liu C, Liu S, Wu R
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 28
- DOI: 10.14218/JCTH.2026.00167
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43022442/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-14

#### 摘要

Hepatitis B virus (HBV) reactivation is a well-recognized complication in patients with lymphoma receiving immunosuppressive therapy, particularly rituximab-containing regimens. We describe a 58-year-old man with diffuse large B-cell lymphoma and an atypical baseline HBV serological profile: hepatitis B surface antibody (anti-HBs) positivity (102.00 U/L), antibody to hepatitis B core antigen negativity, a low-level hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) result (0.35 COI, reported as negative by the local laboratory), and undetectable HBV DNA. Thirteen days after initiation of the fourth cycle of modified R-CHOP (rituximab, cyclophosphamide, pirarubicin, vinorelbine and dexamethasone) chemoimmunotherapy, the anti-HBs titer had decreased to 1.31 S/CO, the HBsAg level to 0.02 S/CO, and HBV DNA was not reassessed until reactivation. Approximately 16 weeks after treatment completion, the patient developed fatigue, anorexia, and jaundice. HBsAg increased from 28.66 S/CO to 8,048.30 IU/mL, hepatitis B e antigen became positive, and HBV DNA increased to 1.94 × 109 IU/mL. Despite treatment with tenofovir alafenamide fumarate and plasma exchange, the patient died of refractory liver failure and hepatic encephalopathy. These findings suggest that atypical serological profiles may not reliably indicate a low risk of severe HBV reactivation during intensive immunosuppressive therapy. Comprehensive pretreatment risk assessment and long-term monitoring of anti-HBs titers, HBsAg, and HBV DNA during and after therapy may facilitate earlier detection and intervention.

### [A] Successful treatment of anti-glomerular basement membrane antibody disease combined with membranous nephropathy using rituximab: a case report.

- PMID: 43016614
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory

- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Frontiers in immunology
- 作者: Cheng Y, Shao G, Shao L, Xie Z, Hu W, Pan B, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.3389/fimmu.2026.1886319
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43016614/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-14

#### 摘要

Anti‑glomerular basement membrane (anti‑GBM) disease is a rare autoimmune disorder that classically manifests as rapidly progressive glomerulonephritis and pulmonary hemorrhage. The coexistence of anti-GBM disease and membranous nephropathy (MN) was first reported in 1976. Although such cases are rare, many reported patients develop progressive kidney injury and poor renal outcomes. We report a young man with sequential-onset dual glomerulopathy. He initially presented with MN alone and achieved partial remission following glucocorticoid and cyclosporine therapy. Three years later, he developed rapidly progressive glomerulonephritis, and kidney biopsy confirmed anti-GBM disease superimposed on pre-existing MN. This sequential pattern differs from the simultaneous onset of both lesions reported in many previous cases. To minimize gonadal toxicity, he received individualized therapy consisting of glucocorticoids, dose-adjusted cyclophosphamide, rituximab, and plasma exchange. He ultimately recovered normal kidney function and had a favorable renal outcome. This case expands the clinical spectrum of overlapping anti-GBM disease and MN and suggests that patients with MN in remission require long-term serial monitoring for early detection of superimposed autoimmune kidney injury. Individualized immunosuppressive strategies tailored to each patient's clinical characteristics are essential to optimize kidney outcomes in such complex glomerular diseases.

### [A] The changing landscape of autoimmune pulmonary alveolar proteinosis.

- PMID: 43014030
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Respiratory medicine and research
- 作者: Jouneau S, McCarthy C
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 16
- DOI: 10.1016/j.resmer.2026.101335
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43014030/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-14

#### 摘要

Pulmonary alveolar proteinosis (PAP) should be suspected when the classic imaging finding of a "crazy paving" pattern is seen on chest CT; however, this appearance can be subtle and PAP may present with a range of varying CT features. The diagnosis is confirmed by identifying eosinophilic extracellular material that stains positively with Periodic Acid-Schiff on bronchoalveolar lavage specimens. The autoimmune form of PAP (aPAP) is confirmed by detecting anti-GM-CSF antibodies in the patient's serum. We propose a novel classification with definite, probable and possible aPAP diagnosis based on various combination of chest CT, BAL and anti-GM-CSF antibodies. Historically, whole-lung lavage has been the first-line treatment and should be performed only in expert centres. Following positive results from recent randomised, placebo-controlled trials, inhaled GM-CSF supplementation with molgramostim or sargramostim is now equally positioned as first line therapy, WLL remaining the preferred option for patients with respiratory failure, particularly at the time of diagnosis. Rituximab and plasmapheresis may be prescribed as third- and fourth-line treatments, respectively, and lung transplantation may be considered for eligible patients with end-stage respiratory failure. A deeper understanding of aPAP pathogenesis has opened new therapeutic avenues, including the use of PPARγ agonists or statins; however, future research must define the optimal long-term treatment strategy and, crucially, establish clear evidence-based criteria for when therapy should be commenced.

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Neuromyelitis optica spectrum disorder (NMOSD) is a severe immune-mediated astrocytopathy most commonly associated with aquaporin-4 immunoglobulin G (AQP4-IgG). Optic neuritis is a common phenotype in pediatric NMOSD, but diagnosis may be delayed when early magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is unrevealing or when visual loss occurs in the setting of systemic autoimmune disease.
CASE PRESENTATION: A 15-year-old Chinese girl with chronic symmetric inflammatory small-joint symptoms and Sjögren-related features presented with severe right visual loss. At the first documented presentation, the right eye had hand-motion vision and a relative afferent pupillary defect, whereas the left eye, despite preserved visual acuity, already showed mild optic disc pallor and marked peripapillary retinal nerve fiber layer (pRNFL) thinning. A non-contrast brain and optic-nerve MRI performed approximately 5-6 weeks after symptom onset was reported as unrevealing. Rheumatoid factor (RF) was positive on two occasions more than 3 months apart, anti-cyclic citrullinated peptide antibody was negative, and hand MRI showed rheumatoid-arthritis-like inflammatory changes. The rheumatologic phenotype was therefore described as chronic RF-positive inflammatory polyarthritis, with RF-positive polyarticular juvenile idiopathic arthritis considered in the pediatric differential diagnosis. Four months later, she developed painful left visual loss. Serum AQP4-IgG was positive by fixed cell-based assay at a titer of 1:320, whereas serum MOG-IgG was negative. She fulfilled the 2015 International Consensus Diagnostic Criteria for AQP4-IgG-positive NMOSD. She received intravenous methylprednisolone for the acute attack, followed by an oral prednisone taper, while methotrexate was continued for the rheumatologic disease. Plasma exchange could not be performed because of local resource limitations, and left-eye visual recovery remained limited. During irregular follow-up, severe bilateral visual impairment developed. Incomplete interval documentation precluded distinction between relapse-related injury and chronic post-inflammatory axonal loss.
CONCLUSIONS: This case illustrates that a nondiagnostic initial non-contrast optic-nerve MRI and normal-range pRNFL thickness in the symptomatic eye do not exclude optic neuritis. Early serum AQP4-IgG testing should be considered in adolescents with severe optic neuritis and systemic autoimmune features. Sustained specialist follow-up and appropriate relapse prevention are required after diagnosis.

### [A] Dengue virus infection and Guillain-Barré syndrome: a systematic review of clinical characteristics, outcomes, and predictors of severity.

- PMID: 41421983
- Domain: Out-of-scope

- 作者: Effiong MG, Yakubu AO, Lawal FI, Ojoh UH, Adeniyi TO
- 发表日期: 2025 Dec 20
- DOI: 10.1186/s12879-025-12428-7
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41421983/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-15

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Dengue virus (DENV) is a mosquito-borne flavivirus causing significant morbidity in tropical and subtropical regions. While not classically neurotropic, DENV has been increasingly associated with neurological complications, including Guillain–Barré syndrome (GBS). This review synthesizes evidence on epidemiology, clinical features, outcomes, and predictors of severity in dengue-associated GBS. METHODS: We systematically searched PubMed and Embase (April 2025) using terms for dengue and GBS. Eligible studies were case reports/series with individual-level data on laboratory-confirmed or clinically diagnosed dengue preceding GBS. RESULTS: Thirty-six publications describing 56 patients from Asia, Latin America, and other endemic regions were included. The mean age was 34.9 years (range 1.5–73), with 57% male. 95% had symptomatic dengue; thrombocytopenia occurred in 43%. Median latency from dengue onset to GBS was 7 days. Motor weakness was universal; facial palsy (32%), bulbar weakness (28.6%), sensory deficits (33.9%), and autonomic dysfunction (18%) were common. Electrophysiology showed AMSAN (33.9%), AIDP (32.1%), and AMAN (12.5%). Albuminocytologic dissociation was present in 62.5% of CSF samples. IVIG was administered in 76.7%, plasmapheresis in 14.3%; 25% required mechanical ventilation. Outcomes were favorable in most: 57% achieved full recovery, 32% partial recovery, and 5.4% mortality. CONCLUSIONS: Dengue-associated GBS resembles other post-infectious forms but shows a high proportion of axonal subtypes. Prognosis is generally good with standard therapy. Although severe cases, particularly those with thrombocytopenia, are more likely to require ICU care. Clinicians in endemic areas should maintain a high index of suspicion for GBS in the post-dengue period to enable timely diagnosis and management.

### [A] Effects of different therapeutic methods on the 90-day prognosis of patients with HBV-ACLF: A systematic review and network meta-analysis.

- PMID: 41430999
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance

- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Medicine
- 作者: Hua Y, Liu H, He Y, Liu S, Xin Y
- 发表日期: 2025 Dec 19
- DOI: 10.1097/MD.0000000000046388
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41430999/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-15

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: The short-term mortality rate of hepatitis B virus related acute-on-chronic liver failure (HBV-ACLF) is relatively high, and the optimal therapeutic method for HBV-ACLF is still controversial. This study aimed to investigate the effects of different therapeutic methods on 90-day prognosis of HBV-ACLF patients.
METHODS: PubMed, Embase, and Cochrane Library were searched from their inception date up to February 2, 2023. RCTs or cohort studies related to the treatment of HBV-ACLF with different therapies were identified. Therapeutic methods focused on plasma exchange (PE), mesenchymal stem cell (MSC), granulocyte colony-stimulating factor (G-CSF), glucocorticoid (GC), double plasma molecular absorption system (DPMAS), and nucleos(t)ide analogues. The primary outcome was the 90-day survival rate. The results for binary variables were calculated using odds ratio (OR), with a corresponding 95% confidence interval (CI).
RESULTS: A total of 16 studies were included in this study, which contained the therapeutic methods of PE, GC, G-CSF, MSC, DPMAS + PE, MSC + PE. Compared to nucleos(t)ide analogues, treatment with MSC + PE, DPMAS + PE, G-CSF, MSC and PE increased the odds of 90-day survival rate (OR = 4.58, 95% CI: 1.95-10.75; OR = 2.95, 95% CI: 1.07-8.12; OR = 2.32, 95% CI: 1.15-4.69; OR = 2.36, 95% CI: 1.14-4.91; OR = 1.91, 95% CI: 1.42-2.77), respectively. The odds of the 90-day survival rate after being treated by MSC + PE were superior to PE (OR = 2.40, 95% CI: 1.05-5.51) and GC treatment (OR = 2.86, 95% CI: 1.01-8.09). MSC + PE exhibited the highest likelihood (0.92) of being the optimal therapeutic method in improving the prognosis of patients with HBV-ACLF. For single-drug regimens, G-CSF (0.58) ranks the highest.
CONCLUSION: This meta-analysis showed that MSC + PE was the most effective therapy in improving the 90-day prognosis, and G-CSF was the potential optimal monotherapy in improving the 90-day prognosis of HBV-ACLF patients.

### [A] Systemic lupus erythematosus in critical care: A systematic review of ICU outcomes and management.

- 发表日期: 2025 Dec 18
- DOI: 10.1016/j.autrev.2025.103911
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/40829709/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-15

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Systemic lupus erythematosus (SLE) often progresses to critical illness requiring intensive care unit (ICU) admission, yet contemporary data on outcomes, prognostic factors, and therapeutic approaches are scattered.
OBJECTIVE: To synthesize recent evidence on causes of ICU admission, mortality predictors, and management strategies in adult SLE.
METHODS: A PRISMA-compliant search of PubMed, Web of Science and Cochrane Library (inception-31 December 2024) retrieved studies enrolling adults (≥18 years) with confirmed SLE treated in ICUs. Two reviewers independently selected articles and extracted data; methodological heterogeneity precluded meta-analysis, so results were narratively synthesized.
RESULTS: Thirty-nine studies met the criteria. Infection (40 %), pulmonary involvement (17 %) and renal flares (13 %) were the most common admission triggers. Reported ICU mortality ranged from 20 % to 82 % but rose sharply when baseline Systemic Lupus Erythematosus Disease Activity Index 2000 (SLEDAI-2K) exceeded 16, approximating 80 % mortality (sensitivity 84 %, specificity 90 %) and outperforming Acute Physiology and Chronic Health Evaluation (APACHE) II and Sequential Organ Failure Assessment (SOFA) in discriminating survivors. Additional independent predictors were APACHE II > 16, need for mechanical ventilation, renal replacement therapy and vasopressor support. Mechanical ventilation (59 %) and vasoactive agents (39 %) were the predominant ICU interventions. Immunomodulatory management was heterogeneous: high-dose corticosteroids and cyclophosphamide were ubiquitous, whereas mycophenolate, antimalarials, intravenous immunoglobulin, plasmapheresis, and extracorporeal membrane oxygenation were reserved for selected scenarios.
CONCLUSIONS: Early recognition of poor prognostic factors and prompt ICU admission are essential to improving outcomes in SLE.

### [A] [18F]FET PET enables histopathology-validated detection of non-contrast-enhancing glioblastoma.

- PMID: 42995172

- 作者: Stürzl R, Kirchleitner SV, Lorenz J, Schmidbauer KJ, Stöcklein V, Quach S, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 06
- DOI: 10.1038/s41467-026-78980-1
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42995172/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-09

#### 摘要

Accurate visualization of glioblastoma is essential for guiding biopsy, resection and radiotherapy. Contrast-enhanced MRI is routine in clinical practice, yet it can miss metabolically active tumor outside areas of blood-brain barrier disruption. In a histopathology-validated cohort with 472 tissue samples of 61 patients with IDH-wildtype glioblastoma, we show that O-(2-[¹⁸F]fluoroethyl)-L-tyrosine PET ([18F]FET PET) depicts tumor tissue significantly better than contrast-enhanced MRI (CE-MRI), especially in regions that show no enhancement on MRI. [18F]FET PET achieves substantially higher sensitivity for tumor detection (91.2% vs 52.6%) and maintains high reliability in non-enhancing tissue (positive predictive value 98.3%), findings that are consistent across newly diagnosed and recurrent disease and in both cohorts undergoing either resection or biopsy. These results indicate that [18F]FET PET complements MRI by revealing MRI-occult tumor burden. Incorporating amino acid PET into routine planning may therefore improve the accuracy of tumor visualization and, ultimately, patient management.

### [A] The blood-brain barrier imports diverse neuroprotective proteins through pathways impaired early in Alzheimer's disease models.

- PMID: 43014307
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 跨领域顶刊启发
- 来源类型: 顶刊扩展
- 来源说明: Cell/Nature/Science生命科学顶刊
- 期刊: Cell
- 作者: Zhou H, Maynard JC, Pennacchio AJ, Apolonio A, Liu X, Yao L, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 11
- DOI: 10.1016/j.cell.2026.10.039
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43014307/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-14

#### 摘要

The blood-brain barrier (BBB) has been defined primarily by what it excludes, yet the scale and function of physiological blood-to-brain protein transport remain largely unknown. Using bioorthogonal proteomics, we find that the healthy BBB samples 191 plasma proteins and permits 92 into the brain parenchyma. Enriched for carrier and metabolic functions, these proteins home to distinct brain cell types, such as apolipoprotein A1 (APOA1) to astrocytes and alpha-1 antitrypsin (SERPINA1) to microglia. This cell-type tropism relies on a BBB-parenchyma receptor hand-off: APOA1 crosses the BBB via SR-B1 and engages astrocytic ATP-binding cassette transporter A1 (ABCA1), a key Alzheimer's risk gene, driving cholesterol efflux that supports synapse integrity. Unlike LPS-induced nonspecific leakage, early-stage 5xFAD mice show selective BBB transport remodeling, with reduced APOA1 and increased SERPINA1 uptake. Hepatic APOA1 overexpression restores APOA1 in the 5xFAD brain, rescuing astrocytic, synaptic, and behavioral deficits. These findings recast the BBB as a selective interface that recruits neuroprotective circulatory proteins for brain health.

### [A] USP28 potentiates endothelial necroptosis via deubiquitinating NMMHC IIA in septic male mice.

- PMID: 43020782
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context

- 期刊: Nature communications
- 作者: Tang J, Hu D, Xu M, Chen H, Gong S, Li F, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 15
- DOI: 10.1038/s41467-026-78666-8
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43020782/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-14

#### 摘要

Endothelial dysfunction contributes to multi-organ injury in sepsis, but the underlying mechanisms remain incompletely understood. Here we show that endothelial ubiquitin-specific protease 28 (USP28) is increased in patients with sepsis and in male mice in a murine polymicrobial sepsis model. Mechanistically, USP28 stabilizes non-muscle myosin heavy chain IIA (NMMHC IIA) by removing K48-linked ubiquitination at K1413. Moreover, NMMHC IIA promotes interferon-α/β receptor 1 (IFNAR1) trafficking to the plasma membrane, enhancing IFNAR1/signal transducer and activator of transcription 1 (STAT1) signaling and endothelial necroptosis. In turn, STAT1 transcriptionally increases USP28 expression, forming a positive feedback loop. Endothelial USP28 deletion or pharmacological inhibition attenuates sepsis-induced multi-organ injury, whereas USP28 overexpression exacerbates it. Conversely, endothelial NMMHC IIA or IFNAR1 overexpression largely abrogates the protection conferred by USP28 deficiency in septic male mice. These findings identify a USP28-NMMHC IIA axis that promotes endothelial necroptosis and multi-organ injury in sepsis through IFNAR1/STAT1 signaling.

- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43003903/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-11

#### 摘要

A barrier to precision medicine for ulcerative colitis (UC) is the inability to predict and mechanistically explain treatment responses. Using patient-reported outcomes (PROs) from a phase 3b comparative trial of 768 participants, we identified four distinct patient response-trajectory groups for vedolizumab (anti-integrin) and adalimumab (antitumor necrosis factor). Validation in a second phase 3 trial of 576 participants linked PRO-defined vedolizumab trajectories to long-term disease complications. Bulk, single cell, and spatial transcriptomics and proteomics identified vascular inflammation as associated with vedolizumab nonresponse through interactions between SPP1+ macrophages and endothelial cells. In two separate real-world cohorts of 4,981 and 3,854 patients with UC, concomitant use of renin-angiotensin system inhibitors (modulators of vascular inflammation) was associated with reduced long-term complications for vedolizumab but not anticytokine therapies. These findings define a response-trajectory-based taxonomy that requires prospective validation for clinical utility and applications for selecting vedolizumab combination therapy in UC.

### [C] β-glucan mitigates necrotizing enterocolitis-induced long-term cognitive impairment by remodeling gut microbiota and restoring tryptophan metabolite IAA-mediated gut-brain signaling.

- PMID: 42997978

- 日报日期: 2026-11-09

#### 摘要

Necrotizing enterocolitis (NEC) is a life-threatening gastrointestinal disorder prevalent in preterm infants, and most NEC survivors suffer from persistent cognitive deficits and neurodevelopmental disorders, with no targeted therapeutic approaches available to date. β-glucan has been documented to regulate gut microbiota, suppress inflammation, and exert neuroprotective effects, yet its efficacy and molecular mechanisms against NEC-associated cognitive dysfunction remain unclear. In this study, we established a mouse NEC model with β-glucan intervention to explore its gut-brain axis protective effects. Behavioral assessments verified that β-glucan significantly rescued NEC-induced cognitive impairments, with comparable therapeutic effects between low and high doses. Multi-omics analyses demonstrated that β-glucan effectively reversed NEC-triggered gut microbiota dysbiosis and restored disrupted tryptophan metabolism, notably elevating the level of indole-3-acetic acid (IAA). Fecal microbiota transplantation and exogenous IAA supplementation recapitulated the neuroprotective phenotypes of β-glucan, which inhibited excessive hippocampal microglial activation and neuroinflammation, and repaired impaired synaptic ultrastructure and plasticity. Further mechanistic validation confirmed that IAA mediated neuroprotection via activating the AHR/MAP2K3/p38 signaling cascade. Collectively, β-glucan alleviates NEC-related cognitive injury through modulating the gut microbiota-tryptophan metabolism-IAA axis and subsequent AHR/MAP2K3/p38 signaling activation. This study elucidates a novel gut‒brain axis regulatory mechanism of β-glucan, providing reliable preclinical evidence for the clinical prevention and treatment of long-term neurodevelopmental complications in NEC survivors.

### [C] Intermittent fasting as a therapeutic strategy in Alzheimer's disease: Understanding of brain-derived neurotrophic factor modulation.

- PMID: 42849557
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence

- 期刊: Physiology & behavior
- 作者: Shrirame D, Tatode A, Dabhade D, Taksande J, Taksande B, Kakade O, et al.
- 发表日期: 2027 Jan 01
- DOI: 10.1016/j.physbeh.2026.115522
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42849557/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-09

#### 摘要

Alzheimer's disease (AD) is a progressive neurodegenerative disease with amyloid-beta (Aβ) plaques, tau tangles, neuroinflammation, and oxidative stress, and it causes cognitive impairment and neuronal loss. The pharmacological treatment is of limited effectiveness, therefore the interest in novel approaches such as intermittent fasting (IF). IF, a nutritional regimen of periodic caloric restriction, has shown a potential to modulate neurodegenerative alterations by metabolic and molecular mechanisms. This review presents the implications of IF in the etiopathogenesis of AD, specifically in controlling brain-derived neurotrophic factor (BDNF). BDNF is a major neurotrophin that plays a key role in synaptic plasticity, neuronal survival, and cognition. Evidence indicates that BDNF is drastically decreased in AD, therefore enhancing disease progression. IF enhances BDNF expression via mechanisms such as improved mitochondrial function, increased ketone body production, and reduced systemic inflammation. Preclinical studies indicate that IF not only upregulates BDNF but also alleviates key AD pathologies, including Aβ deposition and tau hyperphosphorylation. Additionally, clinical data highlight the cognitive benefits of IF and its role in modulating metabolic health, although studies specifically linking IF, BDNF and AD in humans remain limited. This review underscores the need for further research to determine optimal IF protocols and clarify its long-term impact on AD progression. IF's ability to modulate BDNF and other neuroprotective pathways positions it as a promising non-pharmacological intervention for AD management. By bridging metabolic health and neuroprotection, IF offers potential therapeutic synergy for mitigating AD's complex etiopathogenesis.

### [C] Probiotics attenuate AlCl₃-induced Alzheimer's disease via gut-brain axis modulation and suppression of NF-κB/NLRP3 neuroinflammation.

- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42838180/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-09

#### 摘要

Disruptions along the brain-gut-microbiota axis significantly contribute to Alzheimer's disease (AD) pathogenesis. This study evaluated the therapeutic potential of a multi-strain probiotic mixture in mitigating cognitive decline, neuroinflammation, and amyloid pathology in an AD rat model. In the present study, after induction of AD in rats by oral administration of AlCl3 (50 mg/kg) for 28 days, followed by oral treatment with a probiotic mixture (109 log CFU/ml) for 42 days. Behavioral assessments were conducted during the final week. Hippocampal tissues were obtained for histopathological and gene expression analyses and fecal samples were subjected to full-length 16S rRNA gene sequencing via Oxford Nanopore approach. Probiotics significantly improved AD-induced deficits in locomotor activity, exploratory behavior, and spatial recognition memory. Histological analysis revealed marked reductions in hippocampal β-amyloid deposition and substantial restoration of colonic mucosal barrier integrity. Microbiota profiling showed that probiotics reversed dysbiosis, significantly enriching beneficial genera (Blautia, Clostridium, Eubacterium). Blautia abundance was strongly negatively correlated with NF-κB expression. Mechanistically, probiotics suppressed neuroinflammation by down-regulating pro-inflammatory cascades TNF-α, NLRP3, IL-1β, and NF-κB activation. Furthermore, treatment suppressed amyloidogenic processing via decreased APP mRNA and enhanced Aβ clearance through upregulation of LRP1 and modulation of AGER. These findings provide integrative evidence that probiotics exert neuroprotective and immunomodulatory effects, supporting their translational potential as a preventive strategy against AD-related cognitive decline.

### [C] Improved flexible silane/Schiff base coating with better biocompatibility for magnesium vascular implants applications.

- PMID: 42748620
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology

- 发表日期: 2027 Jan
- DOI: 10.1016/j.colsurfb.2026.116167
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42748620/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-09

#### 摘要

Biodegradable magnesium alloys hold promise for next-generation vascular stents, but rapid corrosion in physiological environments can cause fracture failure and adverse inflammatory responses, impeding clinical translation. This study describes a composite coating based on solution-gelation (Sg) and GSH-GPA Schiff base (GP) chemistry. This coating combines a dense siloxane barrier with the release of Schiff base, thereby providing self-healing corrosion protection and immunomodulatory bioactivity. The optimized coating (GP/Sg-0.001) reduces the corrosion current density of ZE21B alloy by three orders of magnitude and maintains structural integrity after 30 days of immersion in Hanks' Balanced Salt Solution, with hydrogen evolution reduced by 79.6% compared to the bare alloy. The coating enables self-healing of barrier function within 72 h upon damage via triggered inhibitor release, while retaining an interfacial adhesion force exceeding 12 N. Schiff base doping markedly improves coating flexibility, as shown by large-bend deformation tests. Beyond corrosion control, this bioadaptive interface selectively promotes endothelial cell adhesion, proliferation and migration, preserves erythrocyte morphology (hemolysis <0.5%), and directs macrophage polarization toward the anti-inflammatory M2 phenotype. This interface promotes the synthesis and release of NO by endothelial cells. This coating combines corrosion protection, flexibility, and bioactivity, enabling magnesium alloys to meet the mechanical demands of complex cerebral microvascular implantation and positioning them as a versatile platform for next-generation cardiovascular implants.

### [C] Effects of maternal docosahexaenoic acid intake on febrile seizures and increased seizure susceptibility after febrile seizures: study using mouse models and human samples.

- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42697338/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-09

#### 摘要

The effects of docosahexaenoic acid (DHA) on seizures and epilepsy remain controversial and appear to depend on the specific context. In this study, we investigated the effects of maternal DHA intake on febrile seizures and postfebrile seizure susceptibility. Mice exposed to DHA during pregnancy showed considerably higher brain DHA and 17β-estradiol (E2) levels than controls, along with a marked delay in the onset of febrile seizures. These effects were abolished by letrozole, an inhibitor of cytochrome P450 family 19 subfamily A, suggesting that the DHA-induced increase in E2 reduces susceptibility to febrile seizures. Maternal DHA intake also suppressed neuroinflammation after febrile seizures and reduced seizure susceptibility later in life following pentylenetetrazol treatment. These protective effects also required E2 synthesis, as they were abolished by letrozole, indicating that the DHA-induced increase in E2 reduces long-term seizure susceptibility even after febrile seizures have occurred. In children with febrile seizures, serum DHA and E2 levels were markedly lower than those in controls. In addition, serum interleukin-1 beta levels were inversely correlated with serum E2 concentrations. Together, DHA can delay febrile seizure onset and attenuate inflammation in an E2-dependent manner. Maternal DHA intake during pregnancy may therefore contribute to healthy brain development in children.

- DOI: 10.1016/j.jconrel.2026.115362
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42753822/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-10

#### 摘要

Atherosclerosis (AS) is a long-term inflammatory disorder marked by dysfunctional glycolipid metabolism, abnormal inflammatory response and intercellular crosstalk. Rosuvastatin (RSV), a first-line drug with lipid-lowering activity, fails to adequately modulate the inflammatory microenvironment of atherosclerotic plaques. The natural active ingredient shikonin (SKN) compensates for this limitation through inhibiting glycolysis and anti-inflammation. However, how to efficiently co-delivery them to the plaques remains challenging. In this study, we engineered a ROS-responsive nanosystem (TK-MLP@SR NPs) containing SKN and RSV. This system enables drug release in the plaque microenvironment with high ROS levels, prolongs circulation time, and achieves active lesion targeting. In vitro assay indicated the function of TK-MLP@SR NPs for inhibiting glycolysis and promoting fatty acid oxidation (FAO) in homocysteine-treated macrophages, thereby inhibiting foam cell formation and alleviating inflammation. Moreover, this nanosystem inhibited phenotype switch of vascular smooth muscle cell by restoring normal macrophage - smooth muscle cell crosstalk. In ApoE-/- mice, the accumulation of TK-MLP@SR NPs in the plaque reduced macrophage infiltration, thereby modulating the inflammatory microenvironment to restore smooth muscle cell function and improve the plaque stability, characterized by less lipid deposition, and increased collagen, along with smaller necrotic cores. Compared with free drugs, this strategy exhibited superior efficacy in regulating the plaque microenvironment by inhibiting lipid deposition. In summary, this work introduces a novel plaque environment-activated prodrug platform, providing valuable insights for clinical translation of AS therapy.

### [C] Venetoclax Elicits Nephrotoxicity Through TLR4/MyD88-Mediated Innate Immune Activation and IFN-γ Signaling.

- PMID: 43003695
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence

- 作者: Akbar A, Jamil S, Alkhoshaiban AS, Alsaleem MA, Hassan HM, Aljohani A, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 09
- DOI: 10.1002/jat.70504
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43003695/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-10

#### 摘要

Venetoclax (VTX) is a selective B-cell lymphoma-2 inhibitor which is used for adults with chronic lymphocytic leukemia. However, it induces severe organ toxicity including renal impairments. The current investigation was conducted to elucidate the nephrotoxic impacts of VTX in dose-dependent manner. Thirty-two male Sprague-Dawley rats were randomly divided into four groups including control (normal saline through oral gavage), VTX-low dose (50 mg/kg/day through oral gavage), VTX-moderate dose (75 mg/kg/day through oral gavage), and VTX-high dose (100 mg/kg/day through oral gavage) for 28 days consecutively. Gene expressions were assessed through qRT-PCR. The levels of inflammatory, apoptotic, and renal function markers were evaluated through ELISA. Administration of VTX caused significant upregulation of Interferon-gamma (IFNG), Interferon gamma receptor (IFNGR1), Signal transducer and activator of transcription 1 (STAT1), Interferon regulatory factor 1 (IRF1), Toll-like receptor 4 (TLR4), Myeloid differentiation primary response 88 (MYD88), Interleukin-1 receptor-associated kinase 1 (IRAK1) and Tumor necrosis factor receptor-associated factor 6 (TRAF6). The activity of catalase (CAT), superoxide dismutase (SOD), glutathione (GSH), glutathione peroxidase (GPx), glutathione S-transferase (GST), glutathione reductase (GSR) and hemeoxygenase-1 (HO-1) were significantly lowered while the levels of reactive oxygen species (ROS) and malondialdehyde (MDA) were augmented in response to VTX exposure in dose-dependent manner. Moreover, VTX resulted in significant increase in the levels of urea, creatinine, uric acid, blood urea nitrogen (BUN), kidney injury marker (KIM-1), neutrophil gelatinase-associated lipocalin (NGAL), N-acetyl glucosamine (NAG), cystatin C, and Endothelin-1 alongside decreased creatinine clearance. In addition, the levels of nuclear factor kappa-B (NF-κB), tumor necrosis factor-alpha (TNF-α), interleukin-1 beta (IL-1β), interleukin-6 (IL-6), and cyclooxygenase-2 (COX-2) were increased following the administration of VTX. Furthermore, the levels of cysteine-aspartic acid protease-9 (Caspase-9), Bcl-2-associated X protein (Bax), and cysteine-aspartic acid protease-3 (Caspase-3) were upregulated while the concentrations of B-cell lymphoma 2 (Bcl-2) were suppressed after VTX intoxication. Besides, VTX exposure induced renal histopathological alterations including glomerular degeneration, tubular necrosis, inflammatory infiltration, vascular congestion, vacuolization of the tubular epithelial cells, tubular dilatation, interstitial oedema and marked disruption of renal architecture in a dose-dependent manner. In silico analysis showed favorable interactions among VTX with TLR4 and STAT1, thereby predicting their involvement as molecular targets in VTX-induced nephrotoxicity. These results show that subacute exposure to VTX causes nephrotoxicity associated with dysregulation of TLR4/MyD88 axis, IFN-γ pathway, oxidative stress, inflammatory and apoptotic profile of renal tissues.

### [C] Is atherosclerosis an autoimmune disease?

- PMID: 43003171
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Cross-domain vascular mechanism with exploratory CCM relevance
- 优先级依据: C: broad, exploratory, low-relevance, or unmatched article
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Autoimmunity reviews
- 作者: Moutsopoulos HM, Mavragani CP
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 09
- DOI: 10.1016/j.autrev.2026.104232
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43003171/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-10

#### 摘要

Atherosclerosis was traditionally viewed as a lipid-storage disorder driven by dyslipidemia and vascular injury. However, accumulating evidence indicates that immune mechanisms are central to disease initiation, progression, and plaque destabilization. Oxidized low-density lipoprotein (oxLDL) and related oxidation-specific epitopes (OSEs), ApoB100 peptides as well as newly identified autoantigens function as modified self-antigens that trigger both innate and adaptive immune responses, linking metabolic stress to chronic vascular inflammation. Innate immune activation is sustained through trained immunity, whereby monocytes and macrophages undergo long-lasting epigenetic and metabolic reprogramming that promotes a persistent pro-inflammatory phenotype. Additional contributors include dendritic cell-mediated antigen presentation, activation of the NLRP3 inflammasome and interleukin-1β signaling pathway, and type I interferon responses, all of which amplify vascular inflammation and plaque progression. Clinical benefits observed with anti-inflammatory therapies targeting these pathways provide evidence for their pathogenic relevance. Adaptive immune responses are characterized by the presence of antigen-experienced T and B lymphocytes within atherosclerotic lesions. oxLDL- and apolipoprotein B (ApoB)-reactive T cells contribute to disease progression through loss of immune tolerance and predominance of pro-inflammatory T helper 1 responses, whereas regulatory T cells exert protective effects. CD8 cytotoxic T cells as well as B cell subsets contribute to the formation of atherosclerotic plaque. Humoral responses against oxLDL generate both atheroprotective and pro-inflammatory antibodies, depending on their specificity and functional properties. Emerging evidence of tertiary lymphoid structures within the arterial wall further supports local antigen-driven immune activation. The accelerated development of atherosclerosis in systemic autoimmune diseases highlights important mechanistic overlap between systemic autoimmunity and vascular pathology. Nevertheless, immune activation in atherosclerosis remains largely initiated by lipid accumulation and vascular injury rather than a primary breakdown of systemic immune tolerance. Atherosclerosis does not appear to require a primary, generalized breakdown of systemic self-tolerance. Nevertheless, ApoB-reactive CD4+ T cells can lose regulatory features during disease progression, indicating impaired antigen-specific immune regulation. Whether, and to what extent, the local arterial environment drives this change remains to be established. Collectively, current evidence supports the concept that atherosclerosis is a chronic immune-mediated inflammatory disease with prominent autoimmune features, providing a rationale for the development of targeted immunomodulatory therapies.

### [C] IL23-axis cytokines and C-reactive protein as dissociable biomarkers of vascular recovery in psoriasis: A hypothesis.

- PMID: 43002317
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Cross-domain vascular mechanism with exploratory CCM relevance
- 优先级依据: C: broad, exploratory, low-relevance, or unmatched article
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Immunologic research
- 作者: Zhou Y
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 09
- DOI: 10.1007/s12026-026-09876-x
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43002317/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-10

#### 摘要

Biologic therapy has transformed psoriasis into a clinically controllable disease, yet skin clearance does not guarantee resolution of the systemic inflammation and vascular dysfunction that drive cardiovascular risk in this population. We hypothesize that interleukin (IL)-23-axis-proximal cytokines and C-reactive protein (CRP) mark two distinct, dissociable inflammatory compartments in psoriasis: IL23-axis-proximal signaling predominantly governs cutaneous disease activity and microvascular endothelial function, whereas CRP integrates a broader cardiometabolic burden and tracks macrovascular recovery. We distinguish IL23-axis-proximal cytokines (IL23, IL17a, IL22), whose production is largely confined to lesional skin, from shared effector cytokines (TNF-α, IFN-γ, IL1, and IL6), which also arise from adipose tissue and other systemic sources. This model generates two falsifiable predictions: (1) IL23 blockade will improve cutaneous microvascular endothelial function in proportion to suppression of IL23-axis-proximal signaling and out of proportion to any change in CRP, and (2) persistent CRP elevation despite adequate skin clearance will identify patients with blunted macrovascular recovery even when IL23-axis-proximal signaling is suppressed. Because IL6 is a node shared by cutaneous and adipose inflammation, the two compartments are coupled, and we therefore state the model in relative rather than absolute terms: quantitative dissociation, not statistical independence. If confirmed, this compartmentalized model would explain why near-complete skin clearance under biologic therapy, including IL23 blockade, does not uniformly normalize cardiovascular risk, and would offer two complementary, mechanism-specific biomarkers, rather than skin clearance alone, for monitoring vascular recovery after psoriasis treatment. We outline the rationale for this hypothesis and a longitudinal biomarker-imaging design capable of refuting it.

#### 摘要

AIMS: Multiple sclerosis (MS) is a progressive neurological autoimmune disorder that affects the central nervous system. Current therapies lack the disease-modifying potential and precision. Astaxanthin is a naturally derived marine compound with potent biological properties and has been reported to mitigate the effects of multiple sclerosis. Herein, we aimed to develop astaxanthin-loaded gold nanoparticles to enhance precision in delivery to the brain by the efficient carrier properties of the gold nanoparticles.
METHODS: In this study, we employed an experimental autoimmune encephalomyelitis (EAE) mouse model of multiple sclerosis and treated for 3 weeks with free astaxanthin (6 mg/kg) and astaxanthin-loaded hollow gold nanoparticles (0.6 mg/kg), a 10-fold lower dose of the drug. The outcomes were evaluated by biochemical, pathological, histological, and hematological parameters.
RESULTS: Our findings indicate that astaxanthin-loaded gold nanoparticles significantly improved the disease outcomes. Our findings suggest that astaxanthin-loaded hollow gold nanoparticles improved oxidative-stress indicators and motor deficits and imparted significant cellular protection in the disease condition.
CONCLUSION: Overall, this study highlights the potential of astaxanthin-loaded hollow gold nanoparticles to improve neurological outcomes in the EAE mice model.

### [C] Butin Ameliorates Atherosclerosis by Regulating the TIMM13-TIMM8A Complex to Alleviate Macrophage Ferroptosis.

- PMID: 43001631
- Domain: Out-of-scope

- DOI: 10.1002/ptr.70495
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43001631/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-10

#### 摘要

Atherosclerosis (AS) progression is heavily driven by iron dyshomeostasis and oxidative stress, yet current therapies fail to effectively resolve these underlying drivers. Ferroptosis exacerbates plaque vulnerability through macrophage dysfunction, but its regulatory nodes remain elusive. This study aims to investigate the role of the mitochondrial translocase complex TIMM13/TIMM8A in atherogenesis and evaluate the natural flavonoid Butin as a targeted inhibitor of this complex. Transcriptomic profiling and network pharmacology identified key targets. Oxidized low-density lipoprotein (Ox-LDL)-treated macrophages and high-fat diet-fed ApoE-/- mice were used for functional validation. Direct target engagement was demonstrated by Cellular Thermal Shift Assay, molecular dynamics simulations, competitive Co-Immunoprecipitation, and site-directed mutagenesis. Ox-LDL promoted aberrant TIMM13 upregulation and TIMM8A suppression, triggering mitochondrial iron overload and lipid peroxidation; this ferroptotic cascade was exacerbated by TIMM13 overexpression or TIMM8A knockdown. We identified Butin as a selective binder of TIMM13 at the Asn34 residue, competitively disrupting the pathological TIMM13-TIMM8A interaction. Consequently, Butin restored the SLC7A11/GPX4 axis and alleviated iron overload and inflammation. In ApoE-/- mice, Butin reduced necrotic cores, enhanced plaque stability, and attenuated vascular ferroptosis, whereas in vivo Asn34 mutation abrogated these benefits. This study identifies the TIMM13/TIMM8A complex as a pivotal mitochondrial gatekeeper of ferroptosis in AS, and demonstrates that Butin acts as a targeted natural inhibitor that halts plaque progression by disrupting this complex and restoring iron homeostasis. These findings provide a compelling mechanistic and translational framework for targeting mitochondrial iron handling in cardiovascular disease management.

### [C] 3D model to study the immunomodulatory functions of adipocyte/T lymphocytes paracrine crosstalk.

- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 09
- DOI: 10.1088/1758-5090/aeba19
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42945576/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-10

#### 摘要

Adipose tissue (AT) dysfunction in obesity involves complex interactions among adipocytes, immune cells, endothelial cells and extracellular matrix components. However, mostin vitrostudies investigating adipocyte-immune interactions rely on simplified two-dimensional systems that incompletely recapitulate human AT physiology. Here, we developed a patient-derived three-dimensional (3D) human adiposphere model to investigate adipocyte-T-cell crosstalk under controlled metabolic conditions. Adipospheres were generated from the stromal vascular fraction of human subcutaneous AT obtained from donors with clinically distinct metabolic profile. Following 3D adipogenic maturation under normogenic or obesogenic conditions, adipospheres retained donor-specific adipogenic and metabolic characteristics during long-term culture, as demonstrated by differential expression of adipogenic markers, insulin-responsive AKT signaling and lipolytic activity. Compared with normogenic maturation condition, obesogenic maturation induced marked alterations in adiposphere morphology, cytokine secretion and metabolic function. In a second step, mature adipospheres were co-cultured with activated blood-derived T cells using a Transwell-based paracrine system. T-cells co-culture further modulated adiposphere inflammatory and metabolic- related responses. Transcriptomic analyses revealed coordinated regulation of immune- and metabolism related pathways, indicating extensive transcriptional remodeling during adipocyte-T-cell crosstalk. Overall, this modular 3D adiposphere platform reproduces key features of human AT immunometabolic interactions while preserving donor-specific responses. The system provides a flexible framework for integrating additional cellular components and may facilitate future mechanistic studies and therapeutic screening approaches targeting obesity-associated metabolic and inflammatory disorders.

### [C] SFRP1 as a therapeutic target in Alzheimer's disease: a druggable molecule involved in amyloid, synaptic, and inflammatory pathology.

- 作者: Bovolenta P, Miaja P, D'ambra E, Martínez-Baños M, Pereyra G
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 08
- DOI: 10.1080/14728222.2026.2760107
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43000671/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-10

#### 摘要

INTRODUCTION: Alzheimer's disease (AD), a leading cause of dementia worldwide, still lacks broadly effective disease-modifying therapies. Recent anti-amyloid monoclonal antibodies highlight both the promise and limitations of amyloid-only strategies. Secreted Frizzled-Related Protein 1 (SFRP1), a Wnt antagonist and endogenous inhibitor of the α-secretase ADAM10, has emerged as a glial- and choroid plexus-derived pathogenic factor promoting amyloidogenic APP processing and accumulation, neuroinflammation, and synaptic dysfunction.
AREAS COVERED: We review SFRP1 regulation of ADAM10-dependent non-amyloidogenic APP processing; its accumulation in amyloid plaques and cerebrospinal fluid of AD patients, independently corroborated by unbiased proteomic studies; its role as an astrocyte-to-microglia amplifier of neuroinflammation; its contribution to synaptic and cognitive decline in transgenic models; and its emerging link to APOEε4. We also discuss antibody- and small-molecule-based neutralization strategies, including pharmacodynamic and stage-dependent data defining its therapeutic window.
EXPERT OPINION: SFRP1 is a mechanistically validated, multifunctional target intersecting amyloid, inflammatory, and synaptic pathways upstream of plaque formation. Clinical translation requires sustained brain exposure without compromising safety, biomarker-guided early intervention, and management of systemic consequences from Wnt and ADAM10 de-repression. Addressing these challenges could establish anti-SFRP1 therapy as a complementary, mechanistically distinct AD treatment.

#### 摘要

Macroautophagy is well-characterized in blood endothelial cells but remains poorly defined in lymphatic endothelial cells (LECs). We previously showed that LEC-specific loss of Atg5 (autophagy-related 5; ATG5[LEC-KO]) impairs lymphocytes' egress from secondary lymphoid organs (SLOs) by disrupting the sphingosine-1-phosphate (S1P) gradient, phenocopying S1P lyase blockade by 4-deoxypyridoxine (DOP). Here, we examined how these interventions affect lung metastasis. Both loss of ATG5 in LECs and DOP treatment suppressed melanoma lung colonization, but through distinct mechanisms. DOP reduced lymphocyte numbers but enhanced effector T cell function, consistent with improved immunosurveillance. Conversely, LEC-autophagy loss increased pulmonary B and CD8 T cell abundance and decreased FLT4/VEGFR3 (FMS-like tyrosine kinase 4) expression in LECs, indicating structural remodeling of the lymphatic network. These findings demonstrate that disruption of S1P metabolism or LEC-autophagy constrains metastasis via divergent modulation of lymphatic and immune compartments, underscoring LEC-autophagy as a non-redundant and targetable regulator of metastatic niche formation.Abbreviations: ATG5: autophagy related 5; DOP: 4-deoxypyridoxine; FLT4/VEGFR3: FMS-like tyrosine kinase 4; ICB: immune checkpoint blockade; KO: knockout; LEC: lymphatic endothelial cell; NK: natural killer; PROX1: prospero homeobox 1; S1P: sphingosine-1-phosphate;SLO: secondary lymphoid organ; SPNS2: SPNS lysolipid transporter 2, sphingosine-1-phosphate; TdLN: tumor-draining lymph node; Teffector: effector T cell; Treg: regulatory T cell; VEGFC: vascular endothelial growth factor C; WT: wild-type.

### [C] Manganese Dioxide Nanozyme-Integrated Aligned Nanofibers Promote Diabetic Wound Repair through Growth Arrest-Specific Protein 6-Mediated Fibroblast-to-Macrophage Communication.

- PMID: 43007787

- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context

#### 摘要

Chronic diabetic wounds remain difficult to treat because excessive reactive oxygen species (ROS), persistent inflammation, and impaired neovascularization collectively hinder tissue repair. Here, we present a bioinspired aligned electrospun nanofiber membrane composed of methacrylated gelatin and o-nitrobenzyl-modified gelatin and functionalized with manganese dioxide (MnO2) nanozymes to regulate the wound microenvironment. Upon hydration and brief ultraviolet irradiation, the membrane forms a conformable nanofibrous hydrogel that closely adapts to irregular wound beds. Among three MnO2 loadings, the formulation containing 180 μg/mL MnO2 was selected as the optimized formulation because it attenuated intracellular ROS while promoting fibroblast proliferation and migration and endothelial angiogenic activity. Mechanistic studies showed that the optimized hydrogel promoted fibroblast secretion of growth arrest-specific protein 6 (Gas6), which attenuated inflammatory macrophage responses and enhanced reparative macrophage responses. Gas6 knockdown weakened these effects, whereas recombinant Gas6 recapitulated them. Single-cell RNA sequencing identified treatment-associated changes in wound cell states and suggested Gas6-associated fibroblast-macrophage communication through computational ligand-receptor analysis. In streptozotocin-induced diabetic mice, the optimized hydrogel accelerated wound closure and enhanced vascularization, re-epithelialization, and collagen remodeling. These findings establish an aligned nanozyme-functionalized nanofibrous hydrogel that links redox regulation to Gas6-associated fibroblast-to-macrophage signaling for diabetic wound repair.

#### 摘要

OBJECTIVES: Pulsed dye laser (PDL) treatment for psoriasis has demonstrated its potential for treatment-free remission periods exceeding 1 year. The mechanism leading to remission is not fully understood, but PDL is known to heat the vasculature in the skin. We hypothesized that heat diffuses from the vasculature to the surrounding environment, including perivascular nerves and T-cells, leading to an interruption of the neurogenic inflammatory cycle that drives psoriasis.
METHODS: We compared histological features of the vasculature, nerves, and leukocyte infiltrate in 120 biopsy sections (80 µm thick) from 20 patients, collected before and after two PDL treatments. The alterations in vasculature were categorized into three distinct classifications: papillary vasculature, reticular vasculature, and total vasculature. Nerve fibers were divided into free epidermal nerve fibers, perivascular nerve fibers around capillaries, and reticular nerves. The densities of these structures, as well as the volume of the epidermal and dermal leukocyte infiltrate, were calculated from the three-dimensional (3D) fluorescence microscopy images.
RESULTS: Two sessions of PDL treatment significantly reduced CD3+ leukocyte infiltrate in the skin. Furthermore, pruritus and epidermal thickness were also significantly reduced. Vascular and nerve fiber density did not significantly differ before and after two treatments.
CONCLUSIONS: Using 3D immunofluorescence microscopy to quantify T-cells, vasculature and nerve fibers, we found that two PDL treatments were associated with a significant reduction in CD3+ T-cells, even in patients whose clinical severity did not improve, while vascular and nerve fiber density remained unchanged. Perivascular heat diffusion and potential alterations in vascular integrity may contribute to this reduction, but this hypothesis requires confirmation in future studies.

### [C] Lymphopenia and Circulating Biomarker Dynamics Associated with Clinical Outcome and Toxicity in Pazopanib-Treated Radioiodine-Refractory Thyroid Cancer: Exploratory Analyses from the Pazothyr Trial.

- DOI: 10.1177/10507256261500974
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43007078/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-11

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Pazopanib showed clinical activity in progressive radioiodine-refractory differentiated thyroid carcinoma (RAIR-DTC), although its use is limited by substantial toxicity not reduced by intermittent treatment. We aimed to identify biomarkers associated with outcome and treatment-related toxicity in these patients.
METHODS: This study reported exploratory results related to the Pazothyr trial (NCT01813136) investigating pazopanib efficacy in RAIR-DTC patients. We evaluated lymphopenia and neutrophil-to-lymphocyte ratio (NLR) from the Pazothyr clinical database (n = 168). Dedicated informed consent for additional plasma and tumor sample collection allowed quantification of plasma angiogenic factors and cytokines at baseline and after one month of pazopanib (n = 111). Immune markers (CD8, FOXP3, CD163, and PDL1) analyzed by immunohistochemistry in archived primary tumors from patients (n = 68) with response or stable disease after 6 months of pazopanib were scored semiquantitatively. Associations with response, progression-free survival (PFS), overall survival (OS), and substantial treatment-related adverse events (TRAEs) were evaluated. Given the exploratory nature of this study, no adjustment for multiple comparisons was performed.
RESULTS: At baseline, moderate and severe lymphopenia were associated with shorter PFS, unlike high NLR. Elevated Vascular Endothelial Growth Factor A (VEGF-A), Interleukin 6 (IL-6), and CXCL8 correlated with poorer PFS. High VEGF-A, hepatocyte growth factor (HGF), IL-6, CXCL8, and CXCL9 were associated with shorter OS. X Early changes in placental growth factor (PlGF), HGF, interleukin 12 subunit p40, and CXCL10 were associated with substantial TRAEs. Multivariable analyses identified baseline severe lymphopenia (hazard ratio [HR] = 2.39 [confidence interval or CI 1.27-4.48], p = 0.007) and high CXCL8 (HR = 2.01 [CI 1.26-3.21], p = 0.004) as independent factors associated with progression. High baseline NLR (HR = 2.26 [CI 1.21-4.22], p = 0.01), HGF (HR = 2.37 [CI 1.33-4.20], p = 0.003), and CXCL8 (HR = 2.20 [CI 1.25-3.87], p = 0.006) were similarly associated with death. One-month increases in PlGF (odds ratio [OR] = 2.49 [CI 1.13-5.50], p = 0.024) and HGF (OR = 2.91 [CI 1.32-6.43], p = 0.008) were independently associated with substantial TRAEs. In patients with tumor samples, Programmed cell Death-Ligand 1 (PDL1)-immune cell score (ICS) >1% (HR = 2.05 [CI 1.15-3.66], p = 0.015) was also associated with progression.
CONCLUSIONS: Baseline lymphopenia and inflammatory plasma biomarkers were associated with clinical outcome, whereas early changes in circulating factors were associated with substantial TRAEs. These exploratory results require prospective validation in RAIR-DTC patients treated with pazopanib or other VEGF receptor-targeting multikinase inhibitors. The relevance of PDL1-ICS expression for anti-PD1/PDL1-based therapies requires dedicated prospective trials.

### [C] HuR Inhibition Attenuates Hypertension and Fibrosis in Chronic Kidney Disease.
