- DOI: 10.1161/HYPERTENSIONAHA.126.26933
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43007060/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-11

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Upregulation of the RNA-binding protein HuR (human antigen R) has been reported in patients with chronic kidney disease (CKD), but its pathogenic role remains incompletely understood. We hypothesized that HuR promotes renal and vascular injury in hypertensive CKD and that pharmacological inhibition of HuR attenuates disease progression. We therefore investigated the role of HuR in deoxycorticosterone acetate (DOCA) plus Ang II (angiotensin II)-induced CKD and evaluated the therapeutic efficacy of the HuR inhibitor, KH3 .
METHODS: Male mice underwent uninephrectomy followed by DOCA+Ang II infusion with 1% NaCl in drinking water and were treated with KH3 or vehicle for 3 weeks. Control mice received saline without DOCA or Ang II.
RESULTS: DOCA+Ang II markedly increased HuR expression in circulating exosomes and kidney tissues, which was attenuated by KH3 treatment. KH3 reduced albuminuria, improved renal function, and attenuated kidney hypertrophy, glomerular, and tubulointerstitial fibrosis. These renoprotective effects were associated with reduced podocyte and tubular injury, decreased macrophage infiltration, and suppression of NF-κB (nuclear factor-kappa B) p65, Nox2 (NADPH oxidase 2), AKT (protein kinase B) phosphorylation, TGFβ1 (transforming growth factor β1), and Wisp1 (WNT1-inducible signaling pathway protein 1) signaling. KH3 also partially lowered arterial blood pressure in DOCA+Ang II-treated mice. Further studies in vascular smooth muscle cells and mesenteric resistance arteries suggested that HuR inhibition may suppress vascular profibrotic signaling associated with SGLT2 (sodium-glucose cotransporter 2).
CONCLUSIONS: HuR is upregulated in renal and vascular tissues in DOCA+Ang II-induced CKD and contributes to renal inflammation, oxidative stress, fibrosis, and vascular dysfunction. Pharmacological inhibition of HuR attenuates renal and vascular injury, supporting HuR as a promising therapeutic target for CKD.

### [C] Collagen-Functionalized hydroxyapatite promotes biointegration and vascularization of xenogeneic bone grafts by modulating macrophage polarization.

- DOI: 10.1177/09287329261506919

- 日报日期: 2026-11-11

#### 摘要

Background: In periodontal and bone-defect reconstruction, bovine-derived hydroxyapatite (HA) is widely utilized as a xenogeneic bone graft material. However, its inherent propensity to elicit a foreign body reaction (FBR) often results in a dense fibrous encapsulation at the host-material interface, thereby impeding early vascular ingrowth and integration associated with new bone formation and limiting its efficacy in complex bone defects.Objective: This study aimed to construct a biomimetic type I collagen (Col) coating on HA (HA + Col) and determine whether it reshapes the host-material immune microenvironment.Methods: Using a rat subcutaneous implantation model, we comparatively evaluated immunomodulatory performance and histological outcomes at an acute inflammatory time point (day 2) and a tissue remodeling time point (day 28).Results: Collagen modification markedly altered the nanoscale surface morphology and effectively suppressed excessive inflammatory cell infiltration at early stages. Immunohistochemical and histological analyses showed that HA + Col was associated with shift of macrophages from a pro-inflammatory M1-like profile (iNOS+) toward a pro-resolving/pro-reparative M2-like profile (CD163+). At day 28, compared with unmodified HA, HA + Col significantly reduced fibrous capsule thickness and increased the neovascular area in subcutaneous tissue. Goldner trichrome staining further confirmed improved soft-tissue integration and collagen deposition quality in the HA + Col group. Collectively, collagen functionalization confers immunomodulatory activity to HA, mitigating FBR and establishing an osteoimmune microenvironment favorable for vascularization and soft-tissue integration in subcutaneous tissue.Conclusion: These findings provide a mechanistic rationale for future evaluation of functionalized bone graft substitutes in bone-defect models.[Formula: see text].

- DOI: 10.1002/advs.78491
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43004969/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-11

#### 摘要

During the early phase of treatment, infected diabetic wounds require rapid bacterial control, whereas subsequent healing benefits from relief of persistent oxidative stress and inflammation, resulting in distinct and partly opposing redox demands over time. We therefore developed a stage-adaptive bilayer hydrogel that separates photodynamic antibacterial therapy from engineered small extracellular vesicle (sEV) delivery. The wound-contacting layer contains chlorin e6 (Ce6), while the ROS-responsive upper layer scavenges ROS and retains miR-146a-enriched sEVs carrying a human CD64-recognition module. The bilayer architecture limited premature release of sEV-associated particles during photodynamic treatment but allowed substantial release thereafter, with key vesicle-associated features preserved after irradiation. In human macrophages, engineered sEVs increased intracellular miR-146a, reduced IRAK1 abundance and NF-κB p65 phosphorylation, decreased CD86, and increased CD206. These effects were partly weakened by CD64 blockade. In infected diabetic wounds, Bil-Gel@miR-sEVs reduced bacterial burden and oxidative stress, suppressed IRAK1/NF-κB-associated inflammatory signaling, shifted macrophage-associated markers toward a repair-associated pattern, and improved epithelial, vascular, and matrix reconstruction. This bilayer design therefore links early infection control with later immunoregulation and tissue repair within one biomaterial.

### [C] Neuromelanins: a link between brain aging and neurodegenerative diseases.

- PMID: 43004772
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Low
- 转化相关性依据: Only generic or out-of-scope keyword overlap
- 优先级依据: C: broad, exploratory, low-relevance, or unmatched article
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Physiological reviews
- 作者: Soni J, Capucciati A, Uttarkar M, Monzani E, Pezzoli G, Cassidy CM, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 09
- DOI: 10.1152/physrev.00036.2025
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43004772/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-11

#### 摘要

Neuromelanins (NMs) accumulate in large amounts in catecholaminergic neurons of the substantia nigra (SN) and locus coeruleus (LC), which selectively degenerate in Parkinson's disease (PD) and Alzheimer's disease (AD). Lower amounts of NMs are also present in other human brain regions. NMs are contained in specialized autolysosomes with lipid bodies, proteins, and metals. Their structure, synthesis, functions, and cellular localization differ from those of melanins in non-nervous tissues. Purified NMs have a complex structure, are composed of melanic moieties, lipids, proteins, and inorganic components, and are insoluble. NMs can exert either a protective or toxic role depending on cellular context. NMs synthesis is driven by cytosolic catecholamines and is a protective process, as it removes excess cytosolic catechols that would otherwise trigger neurotoxicity. NMs can bind reactive/toxic metals, forming stable and non-toxic complexes, thereby playing a neuroprotective role. However, NMs released from degenerating neurons in PD and AD activate microglia, which release reactive species and proinflammatory factors, thereby exacerbating neuroinflammation and neurodegeneration. The major histocompatibility complex class I is present within NM-containing organelles of SN and LC neurons and can bind antigens, presenting them on the neuronal membrane. Subsequently, CD8+ T-cells may target catecholaminergic NM-containing neurons, contributing to neurodegeneration. The loss of NM-containing neurons in PD and AD causes a decrease of NMs concentration within the SN and LC, which can be visualized using magnetic resonance imaging (MRI). NM-sensitive MRI of the SN and LC is increasingly being used for the diagnosis and treatment monitoring of neurodegenerative and psychiatric disorders.

### [C] Measles-like hypermutation of a Newcastle disease virus drives fatal encephalitis during BCMA × CD3 T-cell-redirecting immunotherapy.

- DOI: 10.1007/s00401-026-03116-0
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43013135/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-12

#### 摘要

Newcastle disease virus (NDV or AOaV-1) is an avian paramyxovirus that typically causes only mild, self-limiting disease in humans. Here, we report fatal neuroinvasive AOaV-1 infection in a patient with multiple myeloma receiving maintenance therapy with teclistamab, daratumumab, and lenalidomide following frontline teclistamab-based treatment and autologous stem cell transplantation. Following rapidly progressive brainstem-cerebellar encephalitis and negative conventional microbiological testing, unbiased metagenomic RNA sequencing of a cerebellar biopsy identified AOaV-1 at high intraparenchymal abundance. Viral genome analysis revealed striking A-to-G and U-to-C substitutions selectively concentrated in the matrix gene, consistent with ADAR-mediated RNA editing. Long-read sequencing resolved two genetically distinct, independently hypermutated CNS haplotypes. Post-mortem analysis demonstrated widespread dissemination and continued viral diversification in multiple brain regions, whereas a peripheral urine sample contained a minimally mutated putative ancestral lineage. These findings support peripheral infection followed by CNS seeding and compartmentalized within-host evolution. The selective matrix-gene hypermutation closely parallels persistent measles virus infection in subacute sclerosing panencephalitis, revealing a shared mechanism of paramyxovirus adaptation to the CNS. Profound immunotherapy-associated immune dysfunction may thus create niches enabling normally low-pathogenic zoonotic viruses to persist, evolve, and acquire severe neurovirulence.

- 日报日期: 2026-11-12

#### 摘要

Neurosarcoidosis affects approximately 5-10% of patients with systemic sarcoidosis and may mimic primary psychiatric disease, delaying diagnosis. We report a 52-year-old man with pre-existing schizophrenia admitted with bilateral pulmonary embolism and mediastinal lymphadenopathy. Systemic sarcoidosis was confirmed by endobronchial ultrasound-guided lymph node biopsy and gastric histology. The patient subsequently developed epileptic seizures and a progressive neuropsychiatric syndrome initially attributed to his known psychiatric disorder. Electroencephalography demonstrated diffuse slowing with abundant epileptiform discharges and electro-clinical seizures of left temporal onset; brain MRI showed leptomeningeal involvement with cortical diffusion restriction; cerebrospinal fluid excluded infectious causes and showed an intrathecal immune response. A diagnosis of probable neurosarcoidosis was established, although a seizure-related contribution to the MRI findings could not be excluded. Treatment with high-dose corticosteroids and antiseizure medication was followed by marked clinical improvement and reported resolution of the MRI abnormalities. This case underscores the importance of considering neurosarcoidosis in patients with systemic granulomatous disease who develop new neuropsychiatric symptoms, even when a pre-existing psychiatric diagnosis offers an apparently sufficient explanation.

#### 摘要

27-Hydroxycholesterol (27-OHC) is an oxysterol implicated in neuroinflammation, but its relationship to microglial α-synuclein (α-syn) handling and intracellular signaling in Parkinson's disease (PD) models remains incompletely defined. We combined BV2-cell assays, an rAAV-A53T mouse model, untargeted lipidomics and proteomics, network analyses, molecular docking, pathway immunoblotting, pharmacological AKT inhibition, and a cellular thermal shift assay (CETSA). In BV2 cells, 0.5-2.5 μM 27-OHC did not reduce viability, whereas concentrations ≥ 5 μM were cytotoxic. At 2.5 μM, 27-OHC increased cell-associated α-syn signal after exposure to recombinant α-syn PFFs. Brain lipidomics in the PD model showed accumulation of the broad sterol-lipid class, but did not provide absolute brain 27-OHC concentrations. Network analyses nominated AKT1, IL6, STAT3, and MMP9 as hub genes. In three independent BV2 experiments, PFF and 27-OHC exposure were associated with altered phosphorylation of AKT, STAT3, and NF-κB p65; a PFF × 27-OHC interaction was detected for STAT3 phosphorylation. MK-2206 lowered the cell-associated α-syn/GAPDH signal in the combined-treatment group, although the comparison did not remain significant after adjustment across three pairwise tests. CETSA showed greater soluble AKT1 retention after 27-OHC exposure and a higher thermal-curve area under the curve (paired p = 0.023). These findings support an association between 27-OHC exposure, microglial α-syn handling, and AKT-related signaling in BV2 cells. They do not establish direct 27-OHC-AKT1 binding or an in vivo causal effect of brain 27-OHC on microglia.

#### 摘要

Fractional CO₂ laser is widely used in dermatological treatments; however, excessive local inflammatory responses frequently occur following treatment and may contribute to post-treatment complications, highlighting the importance of controlling cutaneous inflammation. Ganoderma lucidum polysaccharides have been reported to possess various pharmacological activities, including anti-inflammatory effects, but their role in laser-induced skin inflammation remains unclear. In this study, a guinea pig model of fractional CO₂ laser-induced skin inflammation was established, and a Ganoderma lucidum polysaccharide fraction (GLP) was topically applied at different concentrations following laser treatment. Histopathological analysis showed reduced inflammatory cell infiltration after GLP treatment, with the greatest reduction observed in the 80 mg/mL group. RNA sequencing revealed that laser-induced differentially expressed genes (DEGs) were enriched in multiple inflammation-related pathways, including cytokine-cytokine receptor interaction, IL-17, TNF, NF-κB, and Jak-STAT signaling pathways. Furthermore, exploratory transcriptomic analysis suggested that GLP treatment was associated with alterations in multiple inflammation-related pathways. In vitro experiments using lipopolysaccharide-stimulated HaCaT cells, fibroblasts, and human umbilical vein endothelial cells showed that GLP reduced the mRNA expression of several inflammation-related factors. Collectively, these findings provide evidence supporting the anti-inflammatory effects of GLP following fractional CO₂ laser treatment. Clinical trial number: Not applicable.

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Chronic cerebral hypoperfusion (CCH) is a key factor in vascular cognitive impairment, and persistent neuroinflammation plays a central role in its development. Studies suggested that the Mediterranean diet, which features extra virgin olive oil (EVOO) as the primary source of fat, can significantly improve cognitive function. Hydroxytyrosol (HT), a polyphenol found in EVOO, plays a crucial role in protecting cognitive function. The present study aims to investigate the effects of HT on cognition in CCH rats and the underlying mechanisms.
METHODS: The rat CCH model was established by permanent bilateral common carotid artery ligation. Dl-3-n-butylphthalide (NBP) served as the positive control. HT was administered at three dose levels. Cognitive function was assessed and pathological changes in the hippocampus were examined. Levels of apoptosis, neuroinflammation, and myeloid differentiation primary response 88 (MyD88) /NF-κB pathway activity were evaluated via immunofluorescence, enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay, and western blotting.
RESULTS: CCH induced cognitive impairment and increased hippocampal neuronal apoptosis, as well as neuroinflammation and activation of the MyD88/NF-κB pathway in rats. These effects were partially reversed by supplementation with NBP or HT. Notably, the medium dose of HT produced the most favorable results, while the high dose did not demonstrate superior efficacy.
CONCLUSIONS: HT treatment is associated with reduced neuroinflammation and inhibition of the MyD88/NF-κB pathway, which may contribute to the alleviation of CCH-induced cognitive impairment. The optimal effect is observed at the medium dose rather than the high dose.

### [C] An injectable self-healing hydrogel with cytokine-regulating and antioxidant activities for accelerating diabetic wound repair.

- 作者: Yang S, Xi J, HePeng Y, Li C, Zhao Y, Xu Z, et al.

- DOI: 10.1039/d6tb01753c
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42935466/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-12

#### 摘要

Diabetic wounds remain a formidable clinical challenge owing to persistent hyperglycemia, excessive oxidative stress, bacterial infection, and protracted inflammation. Herein, we developed a multifunctional hydrogel, designated QCS/4-FPBA/BC, integrating anti-inflammatory, antibacterial, injectable, and self-healing properties. The hydrogel was constructed using a quaternized chitosan (QCS) crosslinked with 4-formylphenylboronic acid (4-FPBA) via dynamic Schiff base and borate ester bonds, conferring favorable injectability and self-healing capacity, while the intrinsic quaternary ammonium cations of QCS endowed it with inherent antibacterial activity. Baicalein-cerium nanoparticles (BC NPs) were further loaded in situ into the hydrogel network to enable efficient and sustained scavenging of excessive reactive oxygen species (ROS). As a multifunctional platform, the QCS/4-FPBA/BC hydrogel exhibited robust anti-inflammatory activity in LPS-stimulated macrophages, as shown by decreased TNF-α and IL-6 levels and increased IL-10 expression. It also influenced macrophage phenotype, reducing the M1-associated iNOS signal while augmenting the M2-associated CD206 signal. In addition, treatment with the hydrogel upregulated VEGF in HUVECs and elevated collagen I in L929 cells, indicating pro-angiogenic and pro-matrix-synthesis capabilities. With these combined attributes, including ROS scavenging, antimicrobial action, inflammatory cytokine rebalancing, and promotion of vascular and fibroblast functions, the QCS/4-FPBA/BC hydrogel offers a comprehensive strategy for diabetic wound management and holds great promise for improving healing outcomes.

- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Phytotherapy research : PTR
- 作者: Pourbarkhordar V, Rahmani S, Hosseinzadeh H, Mehri S
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 10
- DOI: 10.1002/ptr.70517
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43009438/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-12

#### 摘要

Considering the global prevalence of Alzheimer's disease (AD) and the restrictions of current treatment procedures, natural compounds (such as curcumin, hesperidin, and rutin) have been evaluated as multifaceted therapeutic agents. This review demonstrated that these compounds employ neuroprotective effects primarily through upregulating the brain-derived neurotrophic factor/tropomyosin receptor kinase B/cAMP response element binding protein (BDNF/TrkB/CREB) pathway. After inducing this pathway, they subsequently decrease apoptosis through the regulation of B-cell lymphoma protein 2-associated X/B-cell lymphoma 2 protein (Bax/Bcl2) ratio and the inhibition of apoptosis signal-regulating kinase 1 (ASK1) (e.g., icariin). They also modulate oxidative stress via increasing superoxide dismutase, catalase, and glutathione peroxidase (e.g., hesperidin), and suppress neuroinflammation through the inhibition of nuclear factor kappa-light-chain-enhancer of activated B cells (Nf-κB) and proinflammatory cytokines (e.g., curcumin). These compounds enhance the level of postsynaptic density protein (PSD-95), a key enhancer of neuronal plasticity, improve synaptic signaling (e.g., oleanolic acid), and reduce cognitive disorders by reducing the formation of amyloid beta (Aβ) plaques (e.g., rutin). The phosphatidylinositol 3-kinase/protein kinase B (PI3K/Akt) signaling pathway is modulated (e.g., icariin), and cholinergic neurons are restored (e.g., maslinic acid). Despite the beneficial potential of natural compounds, the extrapolation of their findings to clinical application requires more precise preclinical studies and validated biomarkers.

### [C] Guided precision management of iTTP: optimizing treatment, monitoring, and relapse prevention.

- PMID: 43021537
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Low

- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Expert review of hematology
- 作者: Dong H, Zheng XL
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 12
- DOI: 10.1080/17474086.2026.2763858
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43021537/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-14

#### 摘要

INTRODUCTION: Immune-mediated thrombotic thrombocytopenic purpura (iTTP) is caused by severe deficiency of plasma ADAMTS13 (A Disintegrin And Metalloprotease with Thrombospondin type 1 repeats, 13) activity resulting from acquired autoantibodies against ADAMTS13. Significant progress has been made in recent decades in our understandings of pathogenesis, diagnosis, and management of iTTP.
AREAS COVERED: This review summarizes the key literatures available in PubMed (1925-2026) concerning the diagnosis and management of iTTP, particularly focusing on ADAMTS13-guided therapeutic strategy for prevention of the disease exacerbation or relapse.
EXPERT OPINION: The management of iTTP is moving from a uniform protocol toward guided precision strategies based on dynamic assessment of the disease activity and individual relapse risk. ADAMTS13 monitoring should serve as a central component of short- and long-term cares, enabling timely modification of therapy in acute setting and preemptive intervention before clinical relapse occurs. Multicenter and prospective studies are needed to define the best therapeutic targets, the monitoring intervals, and the intervention thresholds. Integration of the rapid diagnostics, monitoring, and mechanism-based therapies may further reduce mortality, prevent relapse, minimize cumulative toxicity, and improve the long-term outcomes.

### [C] Metabolic syndrome and Alzheimer's disease: Mechanistic links and precision prevention (Review).

- PMID: 43023262
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Low
- 转化相关性依据: Only generic or out-of-scope keyword overlap
- 优先级依据: C: broad, exploratory, low-relevance, or unmatched article
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: International journal of molecular medicine
- 作者: Chen H, Ding T, Zhu Y, Yang M, Wang F, Chen P, et al.
- 发表日期: 2027 Jan
- DOI: 10.3892/ijmm.2026.6051
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43023262/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-14

#### 摘要

Metabolic syndrome (MetS) is associated with increased risk of Alzheimer's disease (AD), however, the majority of previous studies regard MetS as an isolated risk factor and focus on single pathways, lacking systematic understanding of combined effects among multiple metabolic components. The present review systematically summarizes the multifaceted mechanisms by which MetS promotes AD pathology, including insulin resistance and cerebral energy dysregulation, oxidative stress and mitochondrial dysfunction, neuroinflammation and glial cell activation, vascular dysfunction and dysregulation of the renin‑angiotensin system, as well as the central convergent signaling pathway involving glycogen synthase kinase‑3β. The present review further highlights emerging regulatory mechanisms involving the gut‑brain axis, epigenetic reprogramming, sex differences and hormonal effects and senescent microenvironment. By emphasizing the peripheral‑central crosstalk and interactive amplification of MetS components, the present review aimed to position AD as a central manifestation of systemic metabolic disorders in the brain and propose a multi‑domain precision strategy integrating lifestyle intervention, metabolic drug repurposing, gut microbiota regulation, anti‑aging therapy and biomarker‑guided subtype classification for intervention against MetS‑associated AD.

### [C] The intracellular region of truncated neurotrophin receptor TrkB-T1 is sufficient to promote brain glial reactivity, neuroinflammation and neurotoxicity.

- PMID: 43024184
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Low
- 转化相关性依据: Only generic or out-of-scope keyword overlap
- 优先级依据: C: broad, exploratory, low-relevance, or unmatched article
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Molecular biomedicine
- 作者: Ugalde-Triviño L, Serrano MC, Díaz-Guerra M
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 13
- DOI: 10.1186/s43556-026-00650-x
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43024184/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-14

#### 摘要

The development of advanced therapies for stroke, spinal cord injury, and neurodegenerative diseases-primary causes of death, disability and dementia-requires a comprehensive understanding of the interplay between excitotoxicity, aberrant neurotrophic-signalling, glial reactivity, and neuroinflammation. Recent evidence has identified the truncated neurotrophin receptor TrkB-T1 as a potential master regulator of these processes. TrkB-T1 levels increase after stroke whereas the pro-survival full-length isoform (TrkB-FL) decreases. Furthermore, stroke-induced excitotoxicity triggers TrkB-T1 regulated intramembrane proteolysis (RIP), which produces TrkB-T1-ICD, a poorly characterised fragment containing the receptor intracellular domain and a few transmembrane amino acids. This study investigated whether the TrkB-T1 intracellular domain intrinsically contributes to neurotoxicity, reactive gliosis, and neuroinflammation. Our results predict that TrkB-T1 RIP generates a nuclear localization signal in TrkB-T1-ICD that might support nuclear translocation and injury-induced transcriptional regulation in neurons undergoing excitotoxicity. Using cell-penetrating peptides (CPPs), we generated a TrkB-T1-ICD mimetic peptide (Bio-LTT1Ct) containing the TrkB-T1 intracellular region. Bio-LTT1Ct translocated to the nucleus of neurons and astrocytes, provoking cell death and inducing early transcriptional changes in neurons, resembling those triggered by excitotoxicity. In vivo, intranasally administrated Bio-LTT1Ct was brain-permeable and distributed within cortical neurons and astrocytes across both hemispheres. Notably, Bio-LTT1Ct administration was sufficient to promote TrkB isoform imbalance, glial reactivity, and acquisition of proinflammatory profiles, all considered stroke hallmarks. In conclusion, these results imply that TrkB-T1 RIP might be a central mechanism of ischaemic damage and identify the receptor's intracellular region as sufficient to recapitulate stroke-like effects, marking this pathological mechanism as a critical target for therapy.

### [C] miR-665 Ameliorates Neuroinflammation After Intracerebral Hemorrhage by Modulating Myeloid Cell Polarization via Targeting STAT3.

- PMID: 43023471
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Low
- 转化相关性依据: Only generic or out-of-scope keyword overlap

- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: The International journal of neuroscience
- 作者: Huang Z, Yang Y, Sun B, Luo J, Yang Y, Zou D, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 13
- DOI: 10.1080/00207454.2026.2763154
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43023471/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-14

#### 摘要

Background Microglia-mediated neuroinflammation contributes to secondary injury after intracerebral hemorrhage (ICH), but the role of miR-665 remains unclear.Methods An ICH rat model was established. A combination of techniques, including Neurological scoring, brain water content measurement, histology and molecular assays were employed to assess miR-665, the STAT3/NF-κB pathway, myeloid polarization and inflammation. Stereotaxic miR-665 agomir/antagomir delivery verified its function. The targeting relationship between miR-665 and STAT3 was confirmed by a dual-luciferase reporter assay. In vitro experiments were performed in Hemin-stimulated BV2 cells with miR-665 mimics/inhibitor, followed by flow cytometry detection of cell polarization and inflammatory factor analysis. Rescue experiments with STAT3 overexpression were conducted both in vivo and in vitro.Results After ICH, miR-665 was downregulated andinduced excessive activation of the STAT3/NF-κB signaling axis in peri-hematomal tissue, accompanied by M1-like/pro-inflammatory myeloid cell polarization and elevated pro-inflammatory cytokines. Upregulation of miR-665 ameliorated neurological function, reduced brain edema, promoted M2-like/repair-associated polarization, and suppressed inflammation, whereas miR-665 downregulation aggravated injury. Dual-luciferase assay confirmed that miR-665 directly targets STAT3. In vitro, miR-665 overexpression inhibited STAT3 phosphorylation and further suppressed the downstream NF-κB signaling, reversing the Hemin-induced M1-like/pro-inflammatory polarization. STAT3 overexpression counteracted the protective effects of miR-665.Conclusion Downregulated miR-665 after ICH exacerbates neuroinflammation by promoting M1-like/pro-inflammatory myeloid cell polarization via disinhibition of STAT3 and subsequent activation of NF-κB signaling. Upregulating miR-665 exerts neuroprotection by inhibiting STAT3 phosphorylation and facilitating M2-like/repair-associated myeloid cell polarization, highlighting the miR-665/STAT3 regulatory cascade as a promising therapeutic target for post-ICH neuroinflammation.

### [C] Postnatal neuronal GPX4 deficiency exacerbates ferroptotic secondary brain injury after traumatic brain injury and is rescued by ferrostatin-1.

- PMID: 43020442
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Low
- 转化相关性依据: Only generic or out-of-scope keyword overlap
- 优先级依据: C: broad, exploratory, low-relevance, or unmatched article
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Free radical biology & medicine
- 作者: Yu C, Song Y, Zhu M, Shen J, Zhang Y, Wang Y, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 12
- DOI: 10.1016/j.freeradbiomed.2026.11.025
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43020442/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-14

#### 摘要

Ferroptosis has emerged as an important component of secondary injury after traumatic brain injury (TBI), yet the in vivo contribution of neuronal glutathione peroxidase 4 (GPX4), the core lipid hydroperoxide detoxifying enzyme, remains incompletely defined. Here we used 10-12-week-old male Gpx4fl/fl;CamKII-Cre mice to test whether postnatal neuronal GPX4 deficiency increases vulnerability to TBI and whether the ferroptosis inhibitor ferrostatin-1 (Fer-1) provides mechanistic rescue. Because CamKII-Cre-mediated recombination begins during early postnatal development, this model reflects postnatal neuronal GPX4 deficiency and cannot isolate an acute, adult-only loss; accordingly, mechanistic conclusions are grounded in the consistency of biochemical, histological, ultrastructural and pharmacological rescue data rather than in temporal isolation of an adult-onset deletion effect. Controlled cortical impact caused an acute decline in neuronal GPX4, maximal at 24 h, with relative preservation in astrocytes and microglia. Compared with littermate controls, GPX4-nKO mice developed worse neurological deficits, greater brain edema, higher Evans blue extravasation and intracranial pressure, amplified lipid peroxidation, reduced glutathione, increased ACSL4, decreased SLC7A11, more ferroptotic mitochondrial injury, larger lesion volume, enhanced neuroinflammation and poorer long-term behavioral recovery. Fer-1 administered at 1 h and 6 h post-injury lowered malondialdehyde and 4-hydroxynonenal, restored glutathione, reduced dying neuron density and lesion volume, and improved acute and chronic outcomes in GPX4-nKO mice. Together, these data support a mechanistic model in which postnatal neuronal GPX4 deficiency sensitizes the injured brain to ferroptotic secondary damage, while Fer-1 rescue strengthens the conclusion that lipid peroxidation-driven ferroptosis is a major effector pathway downstream of neuronal GPX4 loss after TBI. Ferroptosis involvement is supported by converging evidence, although parallel contributions from other injury mechanisms cannot be fully excluded in this in vivo context.

### [C] Attrition Factors in Patients with Metastatic Melanoma Screened for Tumor Infiltrating Lymphocyte Therapy.

- PMID: 43020329
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence

- 转化相关性依据: Only generic or out-of-scope keyword overlap
- 优先级依据: C: broad, exploratory, low-relevance, or unmatched article
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Transplantation and cellular therapy
- 作者: Pachori A, Liu M, Fell G, Ramsdell L, Yoon CH, Lilley E, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 12
- DOI: 10.1016/j.jtct.2026.11.013
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43020329/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-14

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Following recent regulatory approval, tumor-infiltrating lymphocyte (TIL) therapy has entered the treatment landscape for advanced melanoma, but its delivery requires coordinated multidisciplinary care, including surgical tumor procurement (TIL harvest), cellular manufacturing, lymphodepleting chemotherapy, and high-dose interleukin-2. Real-world attrition across this pathway remains incompletely characterized.
METHODS: We conducted a single-center retrospective study of 59 patients with anti-programmed death 1-refractory metastatic melanoma referred for TIL therapy. Patients were categorized into 4 cohorts: eligible, underwent TIL harvest, and received TIL infusion (cohort A); eligible, underwent TIL harvest, but did not receive infusion (cohort B); eligible, but did not undergo TIL harvest (cohort C); and ineligible (cohort D). We evaluated attrition factors, exploratory predictors of pathway completion, and clinical outcomes among treated patients.
RESULTS: Of 59 referred patients, 25 (42.4%) were in cohort A, 9 (15.3%) in cohort B, 17 (28.8%) in cohort C, and 8 (13.6%) in cohort D. The most common reasons for attrition were rapid disease progression and decline in performance status, occurring both before procurement and after TIL harvest but before infusion. The out-of-specification product rate was 14.7% (5/34 patients who underwent procurement); 2 of these 5 patients did not receive infusion. No baseline factor, including M substage, lactate dehydrogenase (LDH), number of prior treatment lines, treatment immediately before evaluation, or liver or brain metastases, was significantly associated with undergoing TIL harvest. Elevated LDH at TIL evaluation was significantly associated with higher odds of not receiving infusion after procurement (odds ratio, 12.0; 95% CI, 1.22-117.8; P=0.02). Among treated patients, median time from evaluation to TIL harvest was 41 days and from TIL harvest to infusion was 50 days. Among 22 response-evaluable patients, the overall response rate was 40.9%, disease control rate was 59.1%, median progression-free survival was 2.27 months, and median overall survival was 6.11 months.
CONCLUSION: Real-world attrition during TIL therapy was substantial and was driven predominantly by clinical deterioration rather than manufacturing failure alone. These findings support earlier referral, streamlined care pathways, and refined patient selection to improve successful TIL delivery in advanced melanoma.

### [C] Acute Neurotoxicity Syndromes from Immunotherapy: Emergency and ICU Management.

- PMID: 43020124
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Low
- 转化相关性依据: Only generic or out-of-scope keyword overlap

- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Seminars in neurology
- 作者: McEntire CRS, Tschoe C, Manzano GS, Eckhardt CA
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 12
- DOI: 10.1055/a-2979-8424
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43020124/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-14

#### 摘要

Advances in cancer immunotherapy have transformed survival outcomes and driven widespread adoption of these treatments. However, as the population receiving immunotherapy has grown, neurologic complications have increasingly emerged, with rates of 1 to 3% among patients receiving immune checkpoint inhibitors and 26.9% among chimeric antigen receptor (CAR) T cell therapy recipients across all FDA-approved products. This review focuses on the early recognition and acute management of neurologic toxicities associated with immunotherapy, with particular emphasis on immune effector cell-associated neurotoxicity syndrome (ICANS) related to CD19 and BCMA-directed CAR T cell therapies. Herein we synthesize current understanding of clinical phenotypes and risk factors for severe neurotoxicity, and review evidence-based approaches to diagnostic evaluation, including neuroimaging, electroencephalography, and laboratory markers. Practical management strategies for severe cases are discussed, including corticosteroid initiation, cytokine-directed immunomodulatory therapies, exploratory therapies, and neurocritical supportive care for fulminant cerebral edema and status epilepticus. We address less common but high-risk neurologic complications associated with CAR T cell therapy beyond ICANS. Lastly, we touch upon key clinical findings and treatments for acute neurologic complications related to immune checkpoint inhibitors, bispecific T cell engagers, tumor-infiltrating lymphocyte therapy, and engineered T cell receptor therapies.

### [C] Therapeutic Apheresis in Nigeria: Awareness, Knowledge Gaps, and Readiness for Service Expansion Among Healthcare Professionals.

- PMID: 43025865
- Domain: Out-of-scope

- 转化相关性依据: Only generic or out-of-scope keyword overlap

- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Therapeutic apheresis and dialysis : official peer-reviewed journal of the International Society for Apheresis, the Japanese Society for Apheresis, the Japanese Society for Dialysis Therapy
- 作者: Okwuonu CG, Idam E, Anyanwu B, Aloziem Z, Obot-Obot M, Amamchukwu B, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 13
- DOI: 10.1002/1744-9987.70241
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43025865/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-15

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Therapeutic apheresis (TA) is a specialized extracorporeal blood purification modality used in the management of complex hematologic, neurologic, and autoimmune diseases. Despite established global guidelines, its utilization in Nigeria remains largely undocumented. This study assessed awareness, technical knowledge, and institutional readiness for TA among Nigerian healthcare professionals.
METHODS: We conducted an open, cross-sectional online survey of licensed Nigerian healthcare professionals, recruited by convenience and snowball sampling through professional social media networks (WhatsApp, Instagram, X) over a 2-month period from January 29, 2026, to April 1, 2026. Because recruitment used an open, non-probability sampling frame, a conventional response rate could not be calculated; of 180 questionnaires received, 155 (86.1%) met pre-specified completeness and uniqueness criteria and were analyzed. The questionnaire assessed demographics, awareness of TA services, technical knowledge (10-item assessment based on ASFA guideline domains), and perceived barriers.
RESULTS: Respondents were predominantly physicians (63.2%; consultants 22.6%, residents 40.6%), practicing mainly in teaching (47.7%) and private (32.9%) hospitals; 51.6% practiced in a single state (Imo State). Awareness of TPE centers was moderate (45.2%, 95% CI: 37.6%-53.1%), and clinical exposure was low (27.7%, 95% CI: 21.3%-35.2%). The mean knowledge score was 5.10 ± 2.44 of 10. Knowledge level was not significantly associated with professional role, years of experience, or institution type (all p > 0.05), with a borderline, nonsignificant difference by specialty (p = 0.051); nephrology and critical care specialists showed the highest proportion of good knowledge. The leading perceived barriers were lack of equipment (79.4%), poor funding (75.5%), limited awareness (75.5%), and lack of trained personnel (74.2%). Nearly all respondents (95.5%) supported establishing a national TA registry.
CONCLUSION: Nigerian healthcare professionals report substantial gaps in TA awareness and technical knowledge alongside strong readiness to support service expansion. These findings, drawn predominantly from a single geopolitical zone, support targeted investment in structured training, equipment and reimbursement pathways, and a coordinated national apheresis registry, while highlighting the need for confirmatory studies with broader geographic and professional representation.

### [C] RBM47-Mediated Stabilization of PARP1 mRNA Exacerbates Subarachnoid Hemorrhage With RBM47 Transcriptionally Regulated by EGR1.

- PMID: 42963960
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Low

- 作者: Zhang L, Sun J, Chi L, Guo Y, Qu M, Wang Y, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 15
- DOI: 10.1096/fj.202602543RR
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42963960/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-15

#### 摘要

Early brain injury after subarachnoid hemorrhage (SAH) is chiefly marked by neuronal apoptosis and neuroinflammation, while the principal regulating molecules have yet to be identified. RBM47 exhibited considerable upregulation in the cerebral cortex of SAH mice and hemoglobin (Hb)-induced primary cortical neurons. Knockdown of RBM47 with intracerebroventricular injection of small interfering RNA improved neurobehavioral scores (modified Garcia score and beam balance test), mitigated brain edema and SAH grade, and decreased the quantity of FJC-positive and TUNEL-positive neurons in the cortex. Furthermore, RBM47 knockdown suppressed the expression of apoptotic proteins, obstructed HMGB1 nuclear to cytoplasmic translocation, and reduced TNF-α and IL-6 concentrations. In vitro results further validated that RBM47 knockdown diminished Hb-induced neuronal apoptosis and neuroinflammation. The transcription factor EGR1 directly bound to the RBM47 promoter and transcriptionally stimulated RBM47. RBM47 enhanced PARP1 expression by binding to the 3'UTR of PARP1 mRNA. Co-immunoprecipitation demonstrated a connection between PARP1 and HMGB1, facilitating HMGB1 release. We conducted label-free proteomics analysis on primary neurons following PARP1 knockdown and Hb therapy. Differentially expressed proteins were enriched in ADP ribosylation, regulation of necroptosis, and the extrinsic apoptotic pathway. GSEA revealed a large negative enrichment of the neuronal apoptotic process, while pathways associated with mitochondrial activity exhibited highly significant positive enrichment. EGR1 transcriptionally stimulates RBM47, which then enhances HMGB1 release by upregulating PARP1, therefore facilitating neuronal death and inflammation following SAH. PARP1 knockdown alters the mitochondrial protein expression profile and inhibits apoptosis and necroptosis. Targeting the EGR1/RBM47/PARP1/HMGB1 axis may provide an innovative therapeutic approach for SAH.

### [C] Multi-Organ Inflammatory and Endocrine Crosstalk Shapes the Early Brain Response to Combined Burn and Blast Injury.

- PMID: 42952293

- 作者: Schwierling HC, Sengupta S, Noel JG, O'Connell CJ, Potter AS, Adam M, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 15
- DOI: 10.1096/fj.202504681R
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42952293/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-15

#### 摘要

The pathology of concomitant burn and traumatic brain injury remains poorly defined, despite their frequent co-occurrence in military settings and the potential for deleterious synergy. To address this gap, we combined established models of blast-induced mild traumatic brain injury and 15% total body surface area flame burn to approximate a common combat-related trauma and examined how the injuries interact to shape systemic inflammation and brain transcriptional programs. Burn injury induced systemic G-CSF and IL-6 secretion and hematopoietic reprioritization, whereas overlapping brain injury did not substantially alter these systemic effects but magnified brain transcriptional responses relative to single injuries. Bulk RNA-seq profiling at 6 h post-injury revealed enriched gene sets associated with inflammation and endocrine stress in the combined injury group, alongside elevated systemic cytokines and corticosterone; thereby suggesting concurrent immune-endocrine activation. Since our prior work demonstrated burn wound-associated IL-1 signaling induces G-CSF and IL-6 secretion, we tested IL-1 neutralizing antibodies and anakinra as therapies. Treatment reduced G-CSF and IL-6 secretion, improved erythropoiesis, and selectively modulated brain transcriptional changes. Single-nucleus RNA-seq projection mapping predicted that the transcripts altered by combined injury were broadly enriched across vascular and glial cell types. Targeted expression profiling identified a shared brain-liver signature characterized by an IL-1/6-dependent inflammatory response alongside an interleukin-independent stress response. These findings highlight IL-1-responsive inflammatory signals and endocrine stress interactions within a multi-organ network that shapes the early brain transcriptional response to the combined injury.

#### 摘要

PURPOSE: Glioblastoma (GB) and x-ray-based radiation therapy are associated with lymphopenia, which worsens prognosis and impairs antitumor immunity. Proton therapy, with its dosimetric properties that spare healthy brain tissue, is an alternative to reduce radiotoxicity. This study examined the effects of brain irradiation with x-rays or protons on lymphopenia.
METHODS AND MATERIALS: We used GL261-tumor-bearing and tumor-free mice models to evaluate various lymphoid and myeloid cell populations after brain irradiation, assessing volume of brain irradiation, beam orientation, and lymphopenia onset timing via flow cytometry.
RESULTS: In the GB mouse model, both tumor- and radiation-induced lymphopenia were evidenced. Severe lymphopenia, with a 50% depletion of CD45+CD3+ lymphocytes, CD3+CD4+ and CD3+CD8+ T-cell, CD3-B220+ B cells, and CD45+CD11b+ myeloids, CD11b+Ly6C+ monocytes, and CD11b+Ly6G+ neutrophils, occurred as early as 12 hours after x-ray exposure in tumor-free mice. Myeloid subtypes recovered by day 2, whereas lymphocyte recovery was cell type dependent. The use of a lateral x-ray beam failed to alter circulating lymphocyte counts. Brain, cervical lymph nodes, and spleen immune cell quantification revealed a reduction in CD3+CD4+, CD3+CD8+ T and CD3-B220+ B cells post-x-ray exposure. Protons caused a 15% decrease in circulating lymphocytes, with no change in myeloid cells. Proton exposure also reduced CD3+CD4+ T and CD3-B220+ B cells in the cervical lymph nodes but did not affect the spleen. Plasma cytokine levels in the interleukin 1 and macrophage inflammatory protein families correlated with leucocyte changes following exposure to both particle types.
CONCLUSIONS: We describe that proton therapy resulted in less lymphopenia than proton radiation, and propose that the mechanism may be related to biological processes rather than solely decreased exposure of circulating lymphocytes. This may be used to guide the incorporation of immune therapy in GB.
SYNOPSIS: Understanding how brain irradiation affects circulating immunity can inform strategies to improve brain tumor treatment. This involves combining irradiation with immunotherapy and adjusting clinical protocols to restore lymphocytes/boost antitumor T cells.

### [C] Baicalin-Enhanced Microglial Repopulation Suppresses NLRP3 Inflammasome Activation and Promotes Neuroprotection in an LPS-Induced Mouse Model.

- PMID: 43026387
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence

- 转化相关性依据: Only generic or out-of-scope keyword overlap

- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Molecular neurobiology
- 作者: Sasidharan A, Mallick S, Tom VV, Bose B, Somayaji Y, Fernandes R
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 14
- DOI: 10.1007/s12035-026-06315-8
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43026387/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-15

#### 摘要

Microglia are the primary innate immune cells in the brain, essential under physiological conditions for maintaining homeostasis. Chronic activation of microglia is associated with the NLRP3 inflammasome pathway, especially following exposure to lipopolysaccharide (LPS). Microglia can be eliminated using a CSF1R inhibitor such as BLZ945. However, NLRP3 inflammasome markers (NLRP3, ASC, caspase-1, IL-1β, and TNF-α) show increased expression following repopulation. This indicates a persistent inflammatory response. To address this, we orally administered a flavonoid compound baicalin which was extracted from the plant Scutellaria baicalensis, to mice during the microglial repopulation phase. Baicalin restored microglial homeostasis and suppressed NLRP3 inflammasome activation. Improved behavioral outcomes and reduced oxidative stress as a result of baicalin were observed at the molecular level. This study investigated whether microglial depletion with BLZ945 followed by baicalin administration promotes microglial repopulation and reduces neuroinflammation compared to BLZ945 treatment alone. Taken together, this suggests that adjusting microglial repopulation may give a wider window to control neuroinflammation. This suggests that baicalin works alongside BLZ945 to improve neuroprotection, particularly in situations where BLZ945 alone is not enough. Overall, baicalin shows potential as a modulator in chronic neuroinflammatory conditions and may be worth exploring in future research.

### [C] CD14 in glioblastoma and temozolomide resistance: what the evidence does and does not support.

- PMID: 43025709
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Low
- 转化相关性依据: Only generic or out-of-scope keyword overlap
- 优先级依据: C: broad, exploratory, low-relevance, or unmatched article
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Biochemical pharmacology
- 作者: Bibi A, Lee B, Choi J, Son C, Ha K, Ibrahim SO, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 13
- DOI: 10.1016/j.bcp.2026.118616
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43025709/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-15

#### 摘要

Glioblastoma (GBM), designated as central nervous system World Health Organization grade 4, is the most lethal adult brain tumor, with a median overall survival (OS) of 14.6 months despite resection, radiotherapy, and temozolomide (TMZ). The benefit of TMZ is largely confined to the 45 % of GBMs carrying a methylated O6-methylguanine-DNA methyltransferase (MGMT) promoter, where, in 206 assessable patients from the pivotal Phase III trial (reported in 2005), adding TMZ to radiotherapy extended median OS from 15.3 to 21.7 months, whereas MGMT-unmethylated tumors derived no significant benefit; these are historical estimates from a single trial subset. Resistance arises through MGMT-mediated repair of O6-methylguanine, MSH6 loss or mutation affecting mismatch repair, glioma stem cell repopulation, and an immunosuppressive microenvironment enriched with CD14+ myeloid cells, including tumor-associated macrophages (TAMs), monocytes, and monocytic myeloid-derived suppressor cells. CD14 is a glycosylphosphatidylinositol-anchored co-receptor for Toll-like receptor 4 (TLR4), expressed constitutively on monocytes, macrophages, and microglia and also by a subset of glioma cells. Because CD14 has no intracellular domain, it cannot signal independently of a partner receptor; CD14-dependent signaling through TLR4/MD-2 has been proposed to contribute to nuclear factor kappa B (NF-κB) activation, pro-survival transcription, MGMT-related resistance pathways, and immunosuppressive myeloid polarization, although no study has shown this in glioma cells, the link to TMZ resistance remains hypothetical, and CD14 alone does not define an M2-like TAM phenotype. This review synthesizes over 50 publications across CD14 biology in the brain, CD14 expression across the immune and tumor-cell landscape of GBM, CD14-associated inflammatory signaling, TMZ resistance mechanisms, CD14-related influences on TMZ sensitivity, and candidate therapeutic strategies. Of those strategies, only IC14 (atibuclimab) targets CD14 directly; TLR4, NF-κB, signal transducer and activator of transcription 3 and colony-stimulating factor 1 receptor inhibition, TAM repolarization, poly(ADP-ribose) polymerase inhibition, and checkpoint blockade act on the partner receptor, on shared downstream nodes or on the CD14-enriched myeloid compartment, and are therefore described as CD14-associated rather than CD14-targeted. CD14+ myeloid populations are associated with aggressive GBM biology, whereas the proposed glioma-cell-intrinsic CD14 → TLR4/MD-2 → NF-κB → MGMT cascade rests on a single 2003 expression dataset and has never been tested by CD14 loss-of-function, gain-of-function, rescue or lineage-specific in vivo experiments. The purpose of this review is to document that gap and to specify the experiments that would close it, not to advocate CD14 as a therapeutic target.

### [C] Forward programming of human pluripotent stem cells to endothelial cells utilizing ETV2-mRNA delivery with ionizable lipid nanoparticles.

- PMID: 43025678
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Cross-domain vascular mechanism with exploratory CCM relevance
- 优先级依据: C: broad, exploratory, low-relevance, or unmatched article
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Acta biomaterialia
- 作者: Rolland JR, Emrick EC, Erickson SM, Feeney AK, Saha S, Jones JM, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 13
- DOI: 10.1016/j.actbio.2026.11.020
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43025678/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-15

#### 摘要

Human pluripotent stem cells (hPSCs) represent a scalable source of cells for off-the-shelf therapeutics and advanced in vitro tissue models. However, their translational utility is constrained by inefficient differentiation protocols that rely on small molecules, cytokines, or genomic modification. Here, we describe a non-integrating approach that utilizes ionizable lipid nanoparticles (iLNPs) to deliver mRNA encoding the ETV2 transcription factor to efficiently forward reprogram hPSCs to endothelial cells (ECs). This platform achieved transfection efficiencies exceeding 97%, with robust expression of functional mRNA cargo. ETV2 delivery induced rapid endothelial conversion, generating cell populations with average purities greater than 90% based on expression of canonical endothelial surface markers Pecam-1 (CD31) and VE-Cadherin (CD144). Forward programming efficiency depended upon culture parameters including initial seeding density, mRNA dosage, and time after transfection. Functionally, iLNP-ETV2-derived ECs exhibited hallmark endothelial behaviors, including angiogenic network formation in vitro, uptake of acetylated low-density lipoprotein, and inflammatory cytokine responsiveness. Collectively, these findings establish iLNP-mediated ETV2 mRNA delivery as an efficient and non-genome-integrating strategy for forward programming hPSCs to ECs, facilitating their use in tissue engineering, disease modeling, and cell-based therapy applications. STATEMENT OF SIGNIFICANCE: Human pluripotent stem cells are a promising source of cells for regenerative medicine and tissue engineering, but existing differentiation methods often produce low yields and require extensive optimization. In this study, we demonstrate that ionizable lipid nanoparticles can achieve highly efficient mRNA delivery to pluripotent stem cells and enable efficient, non-integrating forward programming to endothelial cells through transient expression of the transcription factor ETV2. This approach generated highly pure endothelial populations that retained key endothelial functions and remained stable during long-term expansion. Importantly, endothelial conversion was maintained across broad ranges of cell seeding densities and mRNA doses, suggesting a robust and scalable manufacturing process. These findings establish a versatile platform for mRNA based cell engineering and stem cell differentiation.

### [C] Research Progress of Low-Intensity Pulsed Ultrasound in Parkinson's Disease.

- PMID: 42907270
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Low
- 转化相关性依据: Only generic or out-of-scope keyword overlap
- 优先级依据: C: broad, exploratory, low-relevance, or unmatched article
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 期刊: Ultrasound in medicine & biology
- 作者: Peng W, Yang Y, Wang J, Wang D
- 发表日期: 2027 Jan
- DOI: 10.1016/j.ultrasmedbio.2026.09.028
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42907270/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-15

#### 摘要

Parkinson's disease (PD) is a progressive neurodegenerative disorder where current pharmacological and surgical interventions, including Deep Brain Stimulation, often fail to reverse neuronal degeneration or halt disease progression. Low-intensity pulsed ultrasound (LIPUS) has emerged as a novel, non-invasive neuromodulation technology with significant therapeutic potential. This review synthesizes current research on the key acoustic parameters, biological mechanisms, and clinical applications of LIPUS in PD. Unlike High-Intensity Focused Ultrasound (HIFU), which relies on thermal ablation to destroy target nuclei (e.g., Vim, STN), LIPUS utilizes low-energy mechanical waves (typically <3 W/cm²) to exert non-thermal regulatory effects without damaging tissue. Mechanisms of action include the modulation of mechanosensitive ion channels (e.g., Piezo1), enhancement of neuroplasticity via brain-derived neurotrophic factor (BDNF), promotion of autophagy, and regulation of microglial phenotypes to reduce neuroinflammation. We further compare LIPUS with HIFU, highlighting the superiority of LIPUS regarding safety, reversibility, and the absence of requirement for MRI-based thermal monitoring. While preliminary clinical studies using wearable LIPUS devices indicate improvements in motor and cognitive functions, a unified consensus on optimal stimulation parameters and targeting strategies is lacking. Future research should focus on large-scale, standardized clinical trials to establish precise protocols for LIPUS as a disease-modifying therapy for PD.

### [C] CD4+ T cells are required for tumor antigen-expressing oHSV glioma therapeutic effect and antigen-specific antibody production.

- 作者: Hernandez-Aguirre I, Kim D, Hedberg J, Martin AK, Kim Y, Perez P, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Dec 17
- DOI: 10.1016/j.omton.2026.201353
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42992029/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-15

#### 摘要

Malignant gliomas are treatment-resistant brain tumors needing new therapies. Virotherapy is an experimental therapy that uses attenuated viruses that conditionally replicate and lyse tumor cells and induce tumor immune therapeutic activity. How oncolytic viruses coordinate different arms of the adaptive immune response and mediate glioma therapeutic efficacy remains incompletely defined. We previously showed that an oncolytic virus expressing a shared tumor antigen, ephrin type-A receptor 2 (EphA2), created EphA2-reactive CD8+ T cell formation. Here, we show that, in addition to this CD8+ response, EphA2-oncolytic herpes simplex virus (oHSV) (C170) elicits a coordinated CD4+ T cell and B cell response with functional capacity contributing to glioma control. C170 promotes a T helper type 1 (Th1) and IL-21+CD4+ T cell population, dendritic cell (DC) activation, and germinal center B cell expansion in the tumor draining lymph nodes and produces activated B cell glioma infiltrates with enhanced antigen-presenting capacity. Depletion studies show that C170 therapeutic activity requires CD4+ T cells and that their loss reduces EphA2-specific antibody production. C170-treated mouse serum has improved complement-dependent cytotoxicity in vitro and can suppress tumor growth in vivo. These findings show that antigen-expressing oncolytic virotherapy induces a CD4+ T cell-dependent tumor therapeutic effect and generates a functional tumor antigen-specific humoral response, providing another potential mechanism contributing to C170 therapeutic efficacy.

### [C] Hypoxic bone marrow mesenchymal stem cell-derived exosomes promote neurovascular repair in ischemic stroke with CXCR4-Associated enhancement.

- PMID: 42785582
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Cross-domain vascular mechanism with exploratory CCM relevance
- 优先级依据: C: broad, exploratory, low-relevance, or unmatched article
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 期刊: Brain research
- 作者: Yu X, Jiang X, Cheng Y, Lin Q, Chen C, Fan M
- 发表日期: 2026 Dec 15
- DOI: 10.1016/j.brainres.2026.150562
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42785582/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-15

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Ischemic stroke is a leading cause of disability and mortality worldwide, with limited therapeutic options for long-term recovery. Bone marrow-derived mesenchymal stem cell (BMSC)-derived exosomes have emerged as a promising cell-free therapy, but their efficacy is often constrained by poor homing efficiency.
METHODS: Hypoxic preconditioning and silencing CXCR4 was done through genetic modification of rat BMSCs. The exosomes were captured and identified through transmission electron microscopy, nanoparticle tracking and western blot. The middle cerebral artery occlusion (MCAO) rat model was established and animals were treated with exosomes derived from normoxic, hypoxic, and CXCR4-ooverexpressed or knock-downed BMSCs. Neurological function, infarct volume, vascular density, apoptosis, and neuroinflammation were assessed through behavioral tests, TTC staining, immunofluorescence, and TUNEL assays.
RESULTS: Hypoxic preconditioning significantly enhanced the therapeutic effects of BMSC-derived exosomes, as evidenced by improved neurological scores, reduced infarct volume, and increased cerebral microvascular perfusion. CXCR4 overexpression further enhanced these hypoxia-associated benefits, whereas CXCR4 knockdown partially attenuated them, suggesting that CXCR4 contributes to the therapeutic effects of hypoxic BMSC-derived exosomes.
CONCLUSION: Hypoxic BMSC-derived exosomes promote neurovascular repair after ischemic stroke, and CXCR4 appears to be an important contributing factor that enhances these therapeutic effects.
